kathaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau adutiyaṭṭhaina aikau? sau aivaṃ pabbajitau samānau aikau arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni| sau aikau gacchati, aikau tiṭṭhati, aikau nisīdati, aikau saiyyaṃ kappaiti, aikau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisati, aikau abhik‍kamati, aikau paṭik‍kamati, aikau rahau nisīdati, aikau caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭhāti, aikau carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaitīti – aivaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau adutiyaṭṭhaina aikau|

kathaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau taṇhāya pahānaṭṭhaina aikau? sau aivaṃ aikau adutiyau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau mahāpadhānaṃ padahantau māraṃ sasainakaṃ namuciṃ kaṇhaṃ pamattabandhuṃ vidhamaitvā ca taṇhājāliniṃ visaritaṃ visattikaṃ pajahi vinaudaisi byantīakāsi anabhāvaṃgamaisi|

‘‘taṇhādutiyau  purisau, dīghamaddhāna saṃsaraṃ|

itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ, saṃsāraṃ nātivattati||

‘‘aitamādīnavaṃ ñatvā, taṇhaṃ dukkhassa sambhavaṃ|

vītataṇhau anādānau, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti||

aivaṃ  sau pac‍caikasambuddhau taṇhāya pahānaṭṭhaina aikau|

kathaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau aikantavītarāgauti aikau? rāgassa pahīnattā aikantavītarāgauti aikau, dausassa pahīnattā aikantavītadausauti aikau, mauhassa pahīnattā aikantavītamauhauti aikau, kilaisānaṃ pahīnattā aikantanik‍kilaisauti aikau| aivaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau aikantavītarāgauti aikau|

kathaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau aikāyanamaggaṃ gatauti aikau? aikāyanamaggau vuc‍cati cattārau satipaṭṭhānā, cattārau sammappadhānā, cattārau iddhipādā, pañ‍cindriyāni, pañ‍ca balāni, satta baujjhaṅgā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau|

‘‘aikāyanaṃ jātikhayantadassī, maggaṃ pajānāti hitānukampī|

aitaina maggaina tariṃsu pubbai, tarissanti yai ca taranti augha’’nti||

aivaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau aikāyanamaggaṃ gatauti aikau|

kathaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau aikau anuttaraṃ pac‍caikasambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti  aikau? baudhi vuc‍cati catūsu maggaisu ñāṇaṃ| pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau vīmaṃsā vipassanā sammādiṭṭhi| sau pac‍caikasambuddhau maggapac‍caikasambuddhau ñāṇapac‍caikasambuddhau ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti bujjhi, ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpa’’nti bujjhi, ‘‘nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatana’’nti bujjhi, ‘‘saḷāyatanapac‍cayā phassau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘phassapac‍cayā vaidanā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘vaidanāpac‍cayā taṇhā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘taṇhāpac‍cayā upādāna’’nti bujjhi, ‘‘upādānapac‍cayā bhavau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘bhavapac‍cayā jātī’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘jātipac‍cayā  jarāmaraṇa’’nti bujjhi; ‘‘avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘nāmarūpaniraudhā saḷāyatananiraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘saḷāyatananiraudhā phassaniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘vaidanāniraudhā taṇhāniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘taṇhāniraudhā upādānaniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘bhavaniraudhā jātiniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘jātiniraudhā jarāmaraṇaniraudhau’’ti  bujjhi; ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti bujjhi, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti bujjhi; ‘‘imai āsavā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘ayaṃ āsavasamudayau’’ti bujjhi…pai॰…  ‘‘ayaṃ āsavaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti bujjhi; ‘‘imai dhammā abhiñ‍ñaiyyā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘imai dhammā pahātabbā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘imai dhammā sacchikātabbā’’ti bujjhi, ‘‘imai dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti bujjhi; chan‍naṃ phassāyatanānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca bujjhi, pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ samudayañ‍ca…pai॰… nissaraṇañ‍ca bujjhi, catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca bujjhi, ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti bujjhi|

atha vā, yaṃ bujjhitabbaṃ anubujjhitabbaṃ paṭibujjhitabbaṃ sambujjhitabbaṃ adhigantabbaṃ phassitabbaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ taina pac‍caikabaudhiñāṇaina bujjhi anubujjhi paṭibujjhi sambujjhi adhigacchi phassaisi sacchākāsīti aivaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau aikau anuttaraṃ pac‍caikasambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti – aikau|

caraiti aṭṭha cariyāyau – iriyāpathacariyā, āyatanacariyā, saticariyā, samādhicariyā, ñāṇacariyā, maggacariyā, patticariyā, laukatthacariyā| iriyāpathacariyāti catūsu iriyāpathaisu| āyatanacariyāti chasu ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu| saticariyāti catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu| samādhicariyāti catūsu jhānaisu| ñāṇacariyāti catūsu ariyasac‍caisu| maggacariyāti catūsu ariyamaggaisu| patticariyāti catūsu sāmañ‍ñaphalaisu| laukatthacariyāti tathāgataisu arahantaisu sammāsambuddhaisu padaisatau pac‍caikasambuddhaisu padaisatau sāvakaisu| iriyāpathacariyā ca paṇidhisampan‍nānaṃ, āyatanacariyā ca indriyaisu guttadvārānaṃ, saticariyā  ca appamādavihārīnaṃ, samādhicariyā ca adhicittamanuyuttānaṃ, ñāṇacariyā ca buddhisampan‍nānaṃ, maggacariyā ca sammāpaṭipan‍nānaṃ, patticariyā ca adhigataphalānaṃ, laukatthacariyā ca tathāgatānaṃ arahantānaṃ sammāsambuddhānaṃ padaisatau pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ padaisatau sāvakānaṃ| imā aṭṭha cariyāyau| aparāpi aṭṭha cariyāyau – adhimuc‍cantau saddhāya carati, paggaṇhantau vīriyaina carati, upaṭṭhapaintau satiyā carati, avikkhaipaṃ karauntau samādhinā carati, pajānantau pañ‍ñāya carati, vijānantau viñ‍ñāṇacariyāya carati| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nassa kusalā dhammā āyāpaintīti – āyatanacariyāya carati| aivaṃ paṭipan‍nau visaisamadhigacchatīti – visaisacariyāya carati| imā aṭṭha cariyāyau|

aparāpi aṭṭha cariyāyau – dassanacariyā ca sammādiṭṭhiyā, abhiraupanacariyā ca sammāsaṅkappassa, pariggahacariyā ca sammāvācāya, samuṭṭhānacariyā ca sammākammantassa, vaudānacariyā ca sammāājīvassa, paggahacariyā ca sammāvāyāmassa, upaṭṭhānacariyā ca sammāsatiyā, avikkhaipacariyā ca sammāsamādhissa| imā aṭṭha cariyāyau|

khaggavisāṇakappauti yathā khaggassa nāma visāṇaṃ aikaṃ hauti adutiyaṃ, aivamaiva sau pac‍caikasambuddhau tak‍kappau tassadisau tappaṭibhāgau| yathā atilauṇaṃ vuc‍cati lauṇakappau, atitittakaṃ vuc‍cati tittakappau, atimadhuraṃ vuc‍cati madhurakappau, atiuṇhaṃ vuc‍cati aggikappau, atisītalaṃ vuc‍cati himakappau, mahāudakakkhandhau vuc‍cati samuddakappau, mahābhiñ‍ñābalappattau sāvakau vuc‍cati satthukappauti; aivamaiva sau pac‍caikasambuddhau tattha tak‍kappau tassadisau tappaṭibhāgau aikau adutiyau muttabandhanau sammā laukai carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaitīti – aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘sabbaisu bhūtaisu nidhāya daṇḍaṃ, avihaiṭhayaṃ añ‍ñatarampi taisaṃ|

na puttamicchaiyya kutau sahāyaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

122.

 saṃsaggajātassa  bhavanti snaihā, snaihanvayaṃ dukkhamidaṃ pahauti|

ādīnavaṃ snaihajaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

saṃsaggajātassa bhavanti snaihāti| saṃsaggāti dvai saṃsaggā – dassanasaṃsaggau ca savanasaṃsaggau ca| katamau dassanasaṃsaggau? idhaikac‍cau passati itthiṃ vā kumāriṃ vā abhirūpaṃ dassanīyaṃ pāsādikaṃ paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgataṃ| disvā passitvā anubyañ‍janasau nimittaṃ gaṇhāti – kaisā vā saubhanā saubhaṇā (syā॰) mukhaṃ vā saubhanaṃ akkhī vā saubhanā kaṇṇā vā saubhanā  nāsā vā saubhanā auṭṭhā vā saubhanā dantā vā saubhanā mukhaṃ vā saubhanaṃ gīvā vā saubhanā thanā vā saubhanā uraṃ vā saubhanaṃ udaraṃ vā saubhanaṃ kaṭi vā saubhanā ūrū vā saubhanā jaṅghā vā saubhanā hatthā vā saubhanā pādā vā saubhanā aṅguliyau vā saubhanā nakhā vā saubhanāti| disvā passitvā abhinandati abhivadati abhipatthaiti anuppādaiti anussarati (ka॰) anubandhati rāgabandhanaṃ – ayaṃ dassanasaṃsaggau|

katamau savanasaṃsaggau? idhaikac‍cau suṇāti – ‘‘asukasmiṃ nāma gāmai vā nigamai vā itthī vā kumārī vā abhirūpā dassanīyā pāsādikā paramāya vaṇṇapaukkharatāya saman‍nāgatā’’ti| sutvā suṇitvā abhinandati abhivadati abhipatthaiti anuppādaiti anubandhati rāgabandhanaṃ – ayaṃ savanasaṃsaggau|

snaihāti dvai snaihā – taṇhāsnaihau ca diṭṭhisnaihau ca| katamau taṇhāsnaihau? yāvatā taṇhāsaṅkhātaina sīmakataṃ sīmakataṃ mariyādikataṃ (syā॰) audhikataṃ pariyantikataṃ pariggahitaṃ mamāyitaṃ – ‘‘idaṃ mama, aitaṃ mama, aittakaṃ mama, aittāvatā mama’’| rūpā saddā gandhā rasā phauṭṭhabbā attharaṇā pāvuraṇā dāsidāsā ajaiḷakā kuk‍kuṭasūkarā hatthigavāssavaḷavā khaittaṃ vatthu hirañ‍ñaṃ suvaṇṇaṃ gāmanigamarājadhāniyau raṭṭhañ‍ca janapadau ca kausau ca kauṭṭhāgārañ‍ca, kaivalampi mahāpathaviṃ taṇhāvasaina mamāyati, yāvatā aṭṭhasatataṇhāvicaritaṃ  – ayaṃ taṇhāsnaihau|

katamau  diṭṭhisnaihau? vīsativatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, dasavatthukā micchādiṭṭhi, dasavatthukā antaggāhikādiṭṭhi| yā aivarūpā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantārau diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ gāhau paṭiggāhau patiṭṭhāhau (ka॰) abhinivaisau parāmāsau kummaggau micchāpathau micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ vipariyāsaggāhau viparītaggāhau vipal‍lāsaggāhau micchāgāhau ayāthāvakasmiṃ yāthāvakanti gāhau, yāvatā dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni – ayaṃ diṭṭhisnaihau|

saṃsaggajātassa bhavanti snaihāti dassanasaṃsaggapac‍cayā ca savanasaṃsaggapac‍cayā ca taṇhāsnaihau ca diṭṭhisnaihau ca bhavanti sambhavanti jāyanti sañ‍jāyanti nibbattanti abhinibbattanti pātubhavantīti – saṃsaggajātassa bhavanti snaihā|

snaihanvayaṃ dukkhamidaṃ pahautīti| snaihauti dvai snaihā – taṇhāsnaihau ca diṭṭhisnaihau ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhāsnaihau…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhisnaihau| dukkhamidaṃ pahautīti idhaikac‍cau kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ carati, vācāya duc‍caritaṃ carati, manasā duc‍caritaṃ carati, pāṇampi hanati, adin‍nampi ādiyati, sandhimpi chindati, nil‍laupampi vilaupampi (syā॰) passa mahāni॰ 170 harati, aikāgārikampi karauti, paripanthaipi tiṭṭhati, paradārampi gacchati, musāpi bhaṇati| tamainaṃ gahaitvā rañ‍ñau dassainti – ‘‘ayaṃ, daiva, caurau āgucārī| imassa yaṃ icchasi taṃ daṇḍaṃ paṇaihī’’ti| tamainaṃ  rājā taṃ paribhāsati| sau paribhāsapac‍cayāpi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ dukkhadaumanassaṃ (syā॰) paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau jātaṃ? tassa snaihapac‍cayā ca nandipac‍cayā ca rāgapac‍cayā ca nandirāgapac‍cayā ca jātaṃ|

aittakainapi rājā na tussati| tamainaṃ rājā bandhāpaiti – andubandhanaina vā raj‍jubandhanaina vā  saṅkhalikabandhanaina vā vaittabandhanaina vā latābandhanaina vā pakkhaipabandhanaina vā parikkhaipabandhanaina vā gāmabandhanaina vā nigamabandhanaina vā raṭṭhabandhanaina vā janapadabandhanaina vā, antamasau savacanīyampi karauti – ‘‘na tai labbhā itau pak‍kamitu’’nti| sau bandhanapac‍cayāpi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau jātaṃ? tassa snaihapac‍cayā ca nandipac‍cayā ca rāgapac‍cayā ca nandirāgapac‍cayā ca jātaṃ|

aittakainapi  rājā na tussati| tamainaṃ rājā tassaiva tassa (syā॰) dhanaṃ āharāpaiti – sataṃ vā sahassaṃ vā satasahassaṃ vā| sau dhanajānipac‍cayāpi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau jātaṃ? tassa snaihapac‍cayā ca nandipac‍cayā ca rāgapac‍cayā ca nandirāgapac‍cayā ca jātaṃ|

aittakainapi rājā na tussati| tamainaṃ rājā vividhā kammakāraṇā vividhāni kammakaraṇāni (ka॰) kārāpaiti – kasāhipi tāḷaiti, vaittaihipi tāḷaiti, aḍḍhadaṇḍaihipi tāḷaiti, hatthampi chindati, pādampi chindati, hatthapādampi chindati, kaṇṇampi chindati, nāsampi chindati, kaṇṇanāsampi chindati, bilaṅgathālikampi karauti, saṅkhamuṇḍikampi karauti, rāhumukhampi karauti, jautimālikampi karauti, hatthapaj‍jautikampi karauti, airakavattikampi airakavaṭṭikampi (syā॰ ka॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.169 karauti, cīrakavāsikampi karauti, aiṇaiyyakampi karauti, baḷisamaṃsikampi karauti, kahāpaṇikampi karauti, khārāpatacchikampi khārāpaṭicchikampi (ka॰) karauti, palighaparivattikampi karauti, palālapīṭhakampi karauti, tattainapi tailaina ausiñ‍cati, sunakhaihipi khādāpaiti, jīvantampi sūlai uttāsaiti, asināpi sīsaṃ chindati| sau kammakāraṇapac‍cayāpi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau jātaṃ? tassa snaihapac‍cayā ca nandipac‍cayā ca rāgapac‍cayā ca nandirāgapac‍cayā ca jātaṃ| rājā imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ daṇḍānaṃ issarau|

sau sakaina kammaina kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| tamainaṃ nirayapālā pañ‍cavidhabandhanaṃ nāma kammakāraṇaṃ karaunti kārainti (syā॰ ka॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 3.267 – tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ hatthai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyai hatthai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ pādai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ dutiyai pādai gamainti, tattaṃ ayaukhilaṃ majjhai urasmiṃ gamainti| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā tippā (syā॰) kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti; na ca tāva kālaṃ karauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau jātaṃ? tassa snaihapac‍cayā ca nandipac‍cayā ca rāgapac‍cayā ca nandirāgapac‍cayā ca jātaṃ|

tamainaṃ  nirayapālā saṃvaisaitvā saṃvaisitvā (syā॰ ka॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 3.267 kuṭhārīhi kudhārīhi (syā॰ ka॰) tacchanti…pai॰… tamainaṃ nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā vāsīhi tacchanti| tamainaṃ  nirayapālā rathai yaujaitvā ādittāya pathaviyā sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya sañ‍jautibhūtāya (syā॰) sāraintipi pac‍cāsāraintipi| tamainaṃ nirayapālā mahantaṃ aṅgārapabbataṃ ādittaṃ sampaj‍jalitaṃ sajautibhūtaṃ āraupaintipi auraupaintipi| tamainaṃ nirayapālā uddhaṃpādaṃ adhausiraṃ gahaitvā tattāya lauhakumbhiyā pakkhipanti ādittāya sampaj‍jalitāya sajautibhūtāya| sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍cati| sau tattha phaiṇuddaihakaṃ pac‍camānau sakimpi uddhaṃ gacchati, sakimpi adhau gacchati, sakimpi tiriyaṃ gacchati| sau tattha dukkhā tibbā kharā kaṭukā vaidanā vaidaiti; na ca tāva kālaṅkarauti yāva na taṃ pāpakammaṃ byantīhauti| aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau jātaṃ? tassa snaihapac‍cayā ca nandipac‍cayā ca rāgapac‍cayā ca nandirāgapac‍cayā ca jātaṃ|

tamainaṃ nirayapālā mahānirayai pakkhipanti| sau khau pana mahānirayau –

catuk‍kaṇṇau catudvārau, vibhattau bhāgasau mitau|

ayaupākārapariyantau, ayasā paṭikuj‍jitau||

tassa ayaumayā bhūmi, jalitā taijasā yutā|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā||

kadariyātapanā kadariyā tāpanā (ka॰) mahāni॰ 170 ghaurā, ac‍cimantau durāsadā|

laumahaṃsanarūpā ca, bhaismā paṭibhayā dukhā||

puratthimāya ca bhittiyā, ac‍cikkhandhau samuṭṭhitau|

ḍahantau  pāpakammantai, pacchimāya paṭihañ‍ñati||

pacchimāya ca bhittiyā, ac‍cikkhandhau samuṭṭhitau|

ḍahantau pāpakammantai, purimāya paṭihañ‍ñati||

dakkhiṇāya ca bhittiyā, ac‍cikkhandhau samuṭṭhitau|

ḍahantau pāpakammantai, uttarāya paṭihañ‍ñati||

uttarāya  ca bhittiyā, ac‍cikkhandhau samuṭṭhitau|

ḍahantau pāpakammantai, dakkhiṇāya paṭihañ‍ñati||

haiṭṭhatau ca samuṭṭhāya, ac‍cikkhandhau bhayānakau|

ḍahantau pāpakammantai, chadanasmiṃ paṭihañ‍ñati||

chadanamhā  samuṭṭhāya, ac‍cikkhandhau bhayānakau|

ḍahantau pāpakammantai, bhūmiyaṃ paṭihañ‍ñati||

ayaukapālamādittaṃ, santattaṃ jalitaṃ yathā|

aivaṃ avīcinirayau, haiṭṭhā upari passatau||

tattha sattā mahāluddā, mahākibbisakārinau|

ac‍cantapāpakammantā, pac‍canti na ca miyyarai mīyarai (ka॰)||

jātavaidasamau kāyau, taisaṃ nirayavāsinaṃ|

passa kammānaṃ daḷhattaṃ, na bhasmā hauti napi masi||

puratthimainapi dhāvanti, tatau dhāvanti pacchimaṃ|

uttarainapi dhāvanti, tatau dhāvanti dakkhiṇaṃ||

yaṃ yaṃ disaṃ padhāvanti, taṃ taṃ dvāraṃ pidhīyati|

abhinikkhamitāsā  tai, sattā maukkhagavaisinau||

na tai tatau nikkhamituṃ, labhanti kammapac‍cayā|

taisañ‍ca pāpakammantaṃ, avipak‍kaṃ kataṃ bahunti||

aitaṃ bhayaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ kutau jātaṃ? tassa snaihapac‍cayā ca nandipac‍cayā ca rāgapac‍cayā ca nandirāgapac‍cayā ca jātaṃ|

yāni ca nairayikāni dukkhāni yāni ca tiracchānayaunikāni dukkhāni yāni ca paittivisayikāni dukkhāni yāni ca mānusikāni dukkhāni, tāni kutau jātāni kutau sañ‍jātāni kutau nibbattāni kutau abhinibbattāni kutau pātubhūtāni? tassa snaihapac‍cayā ca nandipac‍cayā ca rāgapac‍cayā ca nandirāgapac‍cayā ca bhavanti sambhavanti jāyanti sañ‍jāyanti nibbattanti abhinibbattanti pātubhavantīti – snaihanvayaṃ dukkhamidaṃ pahauti|

ādīnavaṃ  snaihajaṃ paikkhamānauti| snaihauti dvai snaihā – taṇhāsnaihau ca diṭṭhisnaihau ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhāsnaihau…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhisnaihau| ādīnavaṃ snaihajaṃ paikkhamānauti taṇhāsnaihau ca diṭṭhisnaihau ca ādīnavaṃ snaihajaṃ paikkhamānau dakkhamānau aulaukayamānau nijjhāyamānau upaparikkhamānauti – ādīnavaṃ snaihajaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘saṃsaggajātassa  bhavanti snaihā, snaihanvayaṃ dukkhamidaṃ pahauti|

ādīnavaṃ  snaihajaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

123.

 mittai suhaj‍jai anukampamānau, hāpaiti atthaṃ paṭibaddhacittau paṭibandhacittau (ka॰) |

aitaṃ bhayaṃ santhavai sandhavai (ka॰) paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

mittai suhaj‍jai anukampamānau, hāpaiti atthaṃ paṭibaddhacittauti| mittāti dvai mittā – agārikamittau ca anāgārikamittau pabbajitamittau (ka॰) aivamuparipi ca| katamau agārikamittau? idhaikac‍cau duddadaṃ dadāti, duc‍cajaṃ cajati, duk‍karaṃ karauti, dukkhamaṃ khamati, guyhamassa ācikkhati, guyhamassa parigūhati pariguyhati (syā॰ ka॰), āpadāsu na vijahati, jīvitampissa atthāya paric‍cattaṃ hauti, khīṇai nātimañ‍ñati – ayaṃ agārikamittau|

katamau anāgārikamittau? idha bhikkhu piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca vattā ca vacanakkhamau ca gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā, nau ca aṭṭhānai niyaujaiti adhisīlai samādapaiti, catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugai samādapaiti, catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ…pai॰… catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ… pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ… pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ… sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ… ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa bhāvanānuyaugai samādapaiti – ayaṃ anāgārikamittau|

suhaj‍jā  vuc‍canti yaihi saha āgamanaṃ phāsu gamanaṃ phāsu ṭhānaṃ phāsu nisaj‍janaṃ nisaj‍jā (ka॰) phāsu sayanaṃ phāsu ālapanaṃ phāsu sal‍lapanaṃ phāsu ul‍lapanaṃ phāsu samul‍lapanaṃ phāsu| mittai suhaj‍jai anukampamānau hāpaiti atthanti mittai ca suhaj‍jai ca sandiṭṭhai ca sambhattai ca sahāyai ca anukampamānau anupaikkhamānau anugaṇhamānau attatthampi paratthampi ubhayatthampi hāpaiti, diṭṭhadhammikampi atthaṃ hāpaiti, samparāyikampi atthaṃ hāpaiti, paramatthampi hāpaiti pahāpaiti parihāpaiti paridhaṃsaiti parivaj‍jaiti parisaj‍jaiti (syā॰) antaradhāpaitīti – mittai suhaj‍jai anukampamānau hāpaiti atthaṃ|

paṭibaddhacittauti dvīhi kāraṇaihi paṭibaddhacittau hauti – attānaṃ vā nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti, attānaṃ vā uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti| kathaṃ attānaṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti? tumhai mai bahūpakārā, ahaṃ tumhai nissāya labhāmi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ| yampi yaipi (ka॰) aivaṃ cūḷani॰ khaggavisāṇasuttaniddaisa 151 mai añ‍ñai dātuṃ vā kātuṃ vā mañ‍ñanti tumhai nissāya tumhai  sampassantā| yampi mai paurāṇaṃ mātāpaittikaṃ nāmagauttaṃ, tampi mai antarahitaṃ| tumhaihi ahaṃ ñāyāmi – asukassa kulupakau, asukāya kulupakauti| aivaṃ attānaṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti|

kathaṃ attānaṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti? ahaṃ tumhākaṃ bahūpakārau, tumhai maṃ āgamma buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā, dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā, saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā, musāvādā paṭiviratā, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā; ahaṃ tumhākaṃ uddaisaṃ daimi, paripucchaṃ daimi, upausathaṃ ācikkhāmi, navakammaṃ adhiṭṭhāmi| atha pana tumhai maṃ ujjhitvā paric‍cajitvā (syā॰) añ‍ñai sak‍karautha garuṃ karautha mānaitha pūjaithāti| aivaṃ attānaṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hautīti – mittai suhaj‍jai anukampamānau, hāpaiti atthaṃ paṭibaddhacittau|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ santhavai paikkhamānauti| bhayanti jātibhayaṃ jarābhayaṃ byādhibhayaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ rājabhayaṃ caurabhayaṃ aggibhayaṃ udakabhayaṃ attānuvādabhayaṃ  parānuvādabhayaṃ daṇḍabhayaṃ duggatibhayaṃ ūmibhayaṃ kumbhilabhayaṃ āvaṭṭabhayaṃ susumārabhayaṃ suṃsumārabhayaṃ (syā॰) ājīvikabhayaṃ asilaukabhayaṃ parisasāraj‍jabhayaṃ madanabhayaṃ bhayānakaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau caitasau ubbaigau utrāsau| santhavaiti dvai santhavā – taṇhāsanthavau ca diṭṭhisanthavau ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhāsanthavau…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhisanthavau| aitaṃ bhayaṃ santhavai paikkhamānauti aitaṃ bhayaṃ santhavai paikkhamānau dakkhamānau aulaukayamānau nijjhāyamānau  upaparikkhamānauti – aitaṃ bhayaṃ santhavai paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘mittai suhaj‍jai anukampamānau, hāpaiti atthaṃ paṭibaddhacittau|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ santhavai paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

124.

 vaṃsau visālauva yathā visattau, puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā|

vaṃsak‍kaḷīrauva vaṃsai kaḷīrauva (ka॰) asaj‍jamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

vaṃsau visālauva yathā visattauti vaṃsau vuc‍cati vaiḷugumbau| yathā vaiḷugumbasmiṃ paurāṇakā vaṃsā sattā vaiḷugumbasmiṃ kaṇṭakā jaṭitā saṃsibbitā (syā॰) visattā āsattā laggā laggitā palibuddhā, aivamaiva visattikā vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau anunayau anuraudhau nandī nandirāgau cittassa sārāgau icchā mucchā ajjhausānaṃ gaidhau  paligaidhau saṅgau paṅkau aijā māyā janikā sañ‍jananī sibbinī jālinī saritā visattikā suttaṃ visaṭā āyūhanī dutiyā paṇidhi bhavanaitti vanaṃ vanathau santhavau sinaihau apaikkhā paṭibandhu āsā āsīsanā āsīsitattaṃ rūpāsā saddāsā gandhāsā rasāsā phauṭṭhabbāsā lābhāsā dhanāsā puttāsā jīvitāsā jappā pajappā abhijappā jappanā jappitattaṃ lauluppaṃ  lauluppāyanā lauluppāyitattaṃ pucchañ‍jikatā sādhukamyatā adhammarāgau visamalaubhau nikanti nikāmanā patthanā pihanā sampatthanā kāmataṇhā bhavataṇhā  vibhavataṇhā rūpataṇhā arūpataṇhā niraudhataṇhā rūpataṇhā saddataṇhā gandhataṇhā rasataṇhā phauṭṭhabbataṇhā dhammataṇhā aughau yaugau ganthau upādānaṃ āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ chadanaṃ bandhanaṃ upak‍kilaisau anusayau pariyuṭṭhānaṃ latā vaivicchaṃ dukkhamūlaṃ dukkhanidānaṃ dukkhappabhavau mārapāsau mārabaḷisaṃ māravisayau māranivāsau mārabandhanaṃ taṇhānadī taṇhājālaṃ taṇhāgaddulaṃ taṇhāsamuddau abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ|

visattikāti kainaṭṭhaina visattikā? visālāti visattikā visatāti visattikā, visaṭāti visattikā, visamāti visattikā, visak‍katīti visattikā, visaṃharatīti visattikā, visaṃvādikāti visattikā, visamūlāti visattikā, visaphalāti visattikā, visaparibhaugāti visattikā| visālā vā pana taṇhā rūpai saddai gandhai rasai phauṭṭhabbai kulai gaṇai āvāsai lābhai yasai pasaṃsāya sukhai cīvarai piṇḍapātai saināsanai gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārai kāmadhātuyā rūpadhātuyā arūpadhātuyā kāmabhavai rūpabhavai arūpabhavai sañ‍ñābhavai asañ‍ñābhavai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñābhavai aikavaukārabhavai catuvaukārabhavai pañ‍cavaukārabhavai atītai anāgatai pac‍cuppan‍nai diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu visatā vitthatāti – visattikāti – vaṃsau visālauva  yathā visattau|

puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhāti| puttāti cattārau puttā – atrajau puttau, khaittajau puttau, din‍nakau puttau, antaivāsikau puttau| dārā vuc‍canti bhariyāyau| apaikkhā vuc‍canti taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlanti – puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā|

vaṃsak‍kaḷīrauva asaj‍jamānauti vaṃsau vuc‍cati vaiḷugumbau| yathā vaiḷugumbasmiṃ taruṇakā kaḷīrakā taruṇakaḷīrā (syā॰) asattā alaggā agadhitā apalivaiṭṭhitā (syā॰) apalibuddhā nikkhantā nissaṭā vippamuttā aivamaiva| saj‍jāti dvai saj‍janā – taṇhāsaj‍janā ca diṭṭhisaj‍janā ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhāsaj‍janā…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhisaj‍janā| tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa  taṇhāsaj‍janā pahīnā, diṭṭhisaj‍janā paṭinissaṭṭhā| taṇhāsaj‍janāya pahīnattā diṭṭhisaj‍janāya paṭinissaṭṭhattā sau pac‍caikasambuddhau  rūpai na saj‍jati saddai na saj‍jati gandhai na saj‍jati rasai na saj‍jati phauṭṭhabbai na saj‍jati kulai…pai॰… gaṇai… āvāsai… lābhai… yasai… pasaṃsāya… sukhai… cīvarai… piṇḍapātai… saināsanai… gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārai… kāmadhātuyā… rūpadhātuyā… arūpadhātuyā… kāmabhavai… rūpabhavai… arūpabhavai… sañ‍ñābhavai… asañ‍ñābhavai… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñābhavai… aikavaukārabhavai… catuvaukārabhavai… pañ‍cavaukārabhavai… atītai… anāgatai… pac‍cuppan‍nai… diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu na saj‍jati na gaṇhāti na bajjhati na  palibajjhati na mucchati; nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharatīti – vaṃsak‍kaḷīrauva asaj‍jamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘vaṃsau visālauva yathā visattau, puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā|

vaṃsak‍kaḷīrauva asaj‍jamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

125.

 migau arañ‍ñamhi yathā abaddhau abandhau (syā॰ ka॰), yainicchakaṃ gacchati gaucarāya|

viñ‍ñū narau sairitaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

migau arañ‍ñamhi yathā abaddhau, yainicchakaṃ gacchati gaucarāyāti| migauti dvai migā – aiṇimigau ca pasadamigau ca| yathā ārañ‍ñikau ārañ‍ñakau (syā॰ ka॰) migau arañ‍ñai pavanai caramānau arañ‍ñai vasamānau (syā॰) vissatthau gacchati vissatthau tiṭṭhati vissatthau nisīdati vissatthau saiyyaṃ kappaiti|

vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau migau arañ‍ñai pavanai caramānau vissatthau gacchati vissatthau tiṭṭhati vissatthau nisīdati vissatthau saiyyaṃ kappaiti| taṃ kissa haitu? anāpāthagatau, bhikkhavai, luddassa| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva  kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ paraṃ (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 1.271 vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti, yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau’|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu andhamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ  gatau pāpimatau’, tiṇṇau laukai visattikaṃ| sau vissatthau gacchati vissatthau tiṭṭhati vissatthau nisīdati vissatthau saiyyaṃ kappaiti| taṃ kissa haitu? anāpāthagatau, bhikkhavai, pāpimatau’’ti – migau arañ‍ñamhi yathā abaddhau, yainicchakaṃ gacchati gaucarāya|

viñ‍ñū narau sairitaṃ paikkhamānauti| viñ‍ñūti paṇḍitau pañ‍ñavā buddhimā ñāṇī vibhāvī maidhāvī| narauti sattau māṇavau pausau puggalau jīvau jāgu jantu indagu manujau| sairīti dvai sairī – dhammaupi sairī puggalaupi sairī| katamau dhammau sairī? cattārau satipaṭṭhānā cattārau sammappadhānā cattārau iddhipādā pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau – ayaṃ dhammau sairī| katamau puggalau sairī? yau iminā sairinā dhammaina saman‍nāgatau, sau  vuc‍cati puggalau sairī| viñ‍ñū narau sairitaṃ paikkhamānauti viñ‍ñū narau sairitaṃ dhammaṃ paikkhamānau, dakkhamānau aulaukayamānau nijjhāyamānau upaparikkhamānauti – viñ‍ñū narau sairitaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘migau arañ‍ñamhi yathā abaddhau, yainicchakaṃ gacchati gaucarāya|

viñ‍ñū narau sairitaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

126.

 āmantanā hauti sahāyamajjhai, vāsai ṭhānai gamanai cārikāya|

anabhijjhitaṃ sairitaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

āmantanā hauti sahāyamajjhai, vāsai ṭhānai gamanai cārikāyāti sahāyā vuc‍canti yaihi saha āgamanaṃ phāsu gamanaṃ phāsu gamanāgamanaṃ phāsu ṭhānaṃ phāsu nisaj‍janaṃ phāsu sayanaṃ phāsu ālapanaṃ phāsu  sal‍lapanaṃ phāsu ul‍lapanaṃ phāsu samul‍lapanaṃ phāsu| āmantanā hauti sahāyamajjhai, vāsai ṭhānai gamanai cārikāyāti sahāyamajjhai vāsaipi ṭhānaipi gamanaipi cārikāyapi attatthamantanā paratthamantanā ubhayatthamantanā diṭṭhadhammikatthamantanā samparāyikatthamantanā paramatthamantanā ubhayatthamantanā (ka॰) ti  – āmantanā hauti sahāyamajjhai, vāsai ṭhānai gamanai cārikāya|

anabhijjhitaṃ sairitaṃ paikkhamānauti anabhijjhitaṃ aitaṃ vatthu bālānaṃ asappurisānaṃ titthiyānaṃ titthayasāvakānaṃ, yadidaṃ – bhaṇḍukāsāyavatthavasanatā| abhijjhitaṃ aitaṃ vatthu paṇḍitānaṃ sappurisānaṃ buddhasāvakānaṃ pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ, yadidaṃ – bhaṇḍukāsāyavatthavasanatā| sairīti dvai sairī – dhammaupi sairī puggalaupi sairī| katamau dhammau sairī? cattārau satipaṭṭhānā  …pai॰… ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau – ayaṃ dhammau sairī| katamau puggalau sairī? yau iminā sairinā dhammaina saman‍nāgatau, sau vuc‍cati puggalau sairī| anabhijjhitaṃ sairitaṃ paikkhamānauti sairitaṃ dhammaṃ paikkhamānau dakkhamānau aulaukayamānau nijjhāyamānau upaparikkhamānauti – anabhijjhitaṃ sairitaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘āmantanā hauti sahāyamajjhai, vāsai ṭhānai gamanai cārikāya|

anabhijjhitaṃ sairitaṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

127.

 khiḍḍā khiṭṭā (ka॰) ratī hauti sahāyamajjhai, puttaisu ca vipulaṃ hauti paimaṃ|

piyavippayaugaṃ vippayaugampi (ka॰) vijigucchamānau, aikau  carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

khiḍḍā ratī hauti sahāyamajjhaiti| khiḍḍāti dvai khiḍḍā – kāyikā ca khiḍḍā vācasikā ca khiḍḍā| katamā kāyikā khiḍḍā? hatthīhipi kīḷanti, assaihipi kīḷanti, rathaihipi kīḷanti, dhanūhipi kīḷanti, tharūhipi kīḷanti, aṭṭhapadaihipi kīḷanti, dasapadaihipi kīḷanti, ākāsaipi kīḷanti, parihārapathaipi kīḷanti, santikāyapi kīḷanti, khalikāyapi kīḷanti, ghaṭikāyapi  kīḷanti, salākahatthainapi kīḷanti, akkhainapi kīḷanti, paṅgacīrainapi kīḷanti, vaṅkakainapi kīḷanti, maukkhacikāyapi kīḷanti, ciṅgulakainapi kīḷanti, pattāḷhakainapi kīḷanti, rathakainapi kīḷanti, dhanukainapi kīḷanti, akkharikāyapi kīḷanti, manaisikāyapi kīḷanti, yathāvaj‍jainapi kīḷanti| ayaṃ kāyikā khiḍḍā|

katamā vācasikā khiḍḍā? mukhabhairikaṃ mukhālambaraṃ mukhaḍiṇḍimakaṃ mukhadaiṇḍimakaṃ (syā॰), mukhadindimakaṃ (ka॰) mukhacalimakaṃ mukhakairakaṃ mukhabhairukaṃ (syā॰) mukhadaddarikaṃ nāṭakaṃ lāsaṃ gītaṃ davakammaṃ| ayaṃ vācasikā khiḍḍā|

ratīti anuk‍kaṇṭhitādhivacanamaitaṃ ratīti| sahāyā vuc‍canti yaihi saha āgamanaṃ phāsu gamanaṃ phāsu gamanāgamanaṃ phāsu ṭhānaṃ phāsu nisaj‍janaṃ phāsu sayanaṃ phāsu ālapanaṃ phāsu sal‍lapanaṃ phāsu ul‍lapanaṃ phāsu samul‍lapanaṃ phāsu| khiḍḍā ratī hauti sahāyamajjhaiti khiḍḍā ca rati ca sahāyamajjhai hautīti – khiḍḍā ratī hauti sahāyamajjhai|

puttaisu  ca vipulaṃ hauti paimanti| puttāti cattārau puttā – atrajau puttau, khaittajau puttau, din‍nakau puttau, antaivāsikau puttau| puttaisu ca vipulaṃ hautiṃ paimanti puttaisu ca adhimattaṃ hauti paimanti – puttaisu ca vipulaṃ hauti paimaṃ|

piyavippayaugaṃ vijigucchamānauti dvai piyā – sattā vā saṅkhārā vā| katamai sattā piyā? idha yassa tai haunti atthakāmā hitakāmā phāsukāmā yaugakkhaimakāmā mātā vā pitā vā bhātā vā bhaginī vā puttau vā dhītā vā mittā vā amac‍cā vā ñātī vā sālauhitā vā, imai sattā piyā|

katamai saṅkhārā piyā? manāpikā rūpā manāpikā saddā manāpikā gandhā manāpikā rasā manāpikā phauṭṭhabbā, imai saṅkhārā piyā| piyavippayaugaṃ vijigucchamānauti piyānaṃ vippayaugaṃ vijigucchamānau aṭṭiyamānau harāyamānauti – piyavippayaugaṃ vijigucchamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘khiḍḍā  ratī hauti sahāyamajjhai, puttaisu ca vipulaṃ hauti paimaṃ|

piyavippayaugaṃ vijigucchamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

128.

 cātuddisau  appaṭighau ca hauti, santussamānau itarītaraina|

parissayānaṃ sahitā achambhī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

cātuddisau appaṭighau ca hautīti| cātuddisauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ, tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina abyāpajjhaina (syā॰) passa dī॰ ni॰ 3.308 pharitvā viharati| karuṇāsahagataina…pai॰… muditāsahagataina…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ, tathā tatiyaṃ…pai॰… abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati| cātuddisau appaṭighau ca hautīti maittāya bhāvitattā yai puratthimāya disāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā appaṭikulā (bahūsu) haunti, yai dakkhiṇāya disāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai pacchimāya disāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai uttarāya disāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai puratthimāya anudisāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai dakkhiṇāya anudisāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai pacchimāya anudisāya  sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai uttarāya anudisāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai haiṭṭhimāya disāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai uparimāya disāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai disāsu vidisāsu sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti; karuṇāya bhāvitattā muditāya bhāvitattā upaikkhāya bhāvitattā yai puratthimāya disāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti…pai॰… yai disāsu vidisāsu sattā tai appaṭikūlā hauntīti – cātuddisau appaṭighau ca hauti|

santussamānau  itarītaraināti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina cīvaraina, itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, na ca cīvarahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ appaṭirūpaṃ (syā॰) āpaj‍jati| aladdhā ca cīvaraṃ na paritassati, laddhā ca cīvaraṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhāpasan‍nau anajjhaupan‍nau (syā॰) ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| tāya ca pana itarītaracīvarasantuṭṭhiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti na paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau patissatau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati pac‍caikasambuddhau paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitau| santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina piṇḍapātaina…pai॰… 

santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina saināsanaina…pai॰… santuṭṭhau hauti itarītaraina gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina, itarītaragilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārasantuṭṭhiyā ca vaṇṇavādī, na ca gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārahaitu anaisanaṃ appatirūpaṃ āpaj‍jati| aladdhā ca gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ na paritassati| laddhā ca gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ agadhitau amucchitau anajjhāpan‍nau ādīnavadassāvī nissaraṇapañ‍ñau paribhuñ‍jati| tāya ca itarītaragilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārasantuṭṭhiyā naivattānuk‍kaṃsaiti na paraṃ vambhaiti| yau hi tattha dakkhau analasau sampajānau patissatau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati pac‍caikasambuddhau paurāṇai aggañ‍ñai ariyavaṃsai ṭhitauti – santussamānau itarītaraina|

parissayānaṃ  sahitā achambhīti| parissayāti dvai parissayā – pākaṭaparissayā ca paṭicchan‍naparissayā ca| katamai pākaṭaparissayā? sīhā byagghā dīpī acchā taracchā kaukā mahiṃsā gaumahisā (syā॰) mahāni॰ 5 hatthī ahī vicchikā satapadī, caurā vā assu mānavā vā katakammā vā akatakammā vā, cakkhuraugau sautaraugau ghānaraugau jivhāraugau kāyaraugau sīsaraugau kaṇṇaraugau mukharaugau dantaraugau kāsau sāsau pināsau ḍāhau jarau kucchiraugau mucchā pakkhandikā sūlā visūcikā kuṭṭhaṃ gaṇḍau kilāsau sausau apamārau daddu kaṇḍu kacchu rakhasā vitacchikā lauhitapittaṃ madhumaihau aṃsā piḷakā bhagandalā, pittasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā saimhasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā vātasamuṭṭhānā ābādhā san‍nipātikā ābādhā utupariṇāmajā ābādhā visamaparihārajā  ābādhā aupak‍kamikā ābādhā kammavipākajā ābādhā, sītaṃ uṇhaṃ jighacchā  pipāsā uc‍cārau passāvau ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassā iti vā| imai vuc‍canti pākaṭaparissayā|

katamai paṭicchan‍naparissayā? kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ rāgau dausau mauhau kaudhau upanāhau makkhau paḷāsau issā macchariyaṃ māyā sāṭhaiyyaṃ thambhau sārambhau mānau atimānau madau pamādau, sabbai kilaisā sabbai  duc‍caritā sabbai darathā sabbai pariḷāhā sabbai santāpā sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārā| imai vuc‍canti paṭicchan‍naparissayā|

parissayāti kainaṭṭhaina parissayā? parisahantīti parissayā, parihānāya saṃvattantīti parissayā, tatrāsayāti parissayā| kathaṃ parisahantīti parissayā? tai parissayā taṃ puggalaṃ sahanti parisahanti abhibhavanti ajjhauttharanti pariyādiyanti maddanti| aivaṃ parisahantīti – parissayā|

kathaṃ parihānāya saṃvattantīti parissayā? tai parissayā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ antarāyāya parihānāya saṃvattanti| katamaisaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ? sammāpaṭipadāya anulaumapaṭipadāya apac‍canīkapaṭipadāya anvatthapaṭipadāya dhammānudhammapaṭipadāya sīlaisu paripūrakāritāya indriyaisu guttadvāratāya bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutāya jāgariyānuyaugassa satisampajañ‍ñassa catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ bhāvanānuyaugassa, catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ… catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ… pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ… pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ… sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ… ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa bhāvanānuyaugassa – imaisaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ antarāyāya parihānāya saṃvattanti| aivaṃ parihānāya saṃvattantīti – parissayā|

kathaṃ tatrāsayāti parissayā? tatthaitai tatra tai (ka॰) pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppaj‍janti attabhāvasan‍nissayā| yathā bilai bilāsayā pāṇā sayanti, dakai dakāsayā udakai udakāsayā (syā॰) pāṇā sayanti, vanai vanāsayā pāṇā sayanti, rukkhai rukkhāsayā pāṇā sayanti; aivamaiva tatthaitai pāpakā akusalā dhammā uppaj‍janti attabhāvasan‍nissayāti| aivampi tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

vuttañhaitaṃ  bhagavatā – ‘‘sāntaivāsikau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sācariyakau dukkhaṃ na phāsu viharati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sāntaivāsikau sācariyakau dukkhaṃ na phāsu  viharati? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā uppaj‍janti yai pāpakā akusalā dhammā, sarasaṅkappā saṃñ‍ñaujanīyā sañ‍ñaujanikā (ka॰), tyassa antau vasanti anvāssavanti pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sāntaivāsikau vuc‍cati| taina samudācaraina samudācaranti naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sācariyakauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā…pai॰… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā…pai॰… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya uppaj‍janti yai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sarasaṅkappā saṃyaujanīyā, tyassa antau vasanti anvāssavanti pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sāntaivāsikauti vuc‍cati| taina samudācaraina samudācaranti naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammāti| tasmā sācariyakauti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sāntaivāsikau sācariyakau dukkhaṃ na phāsu viharatī’’ti| aivampi, tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, antarāmalā antarāamittā antarāsapattā antarāvadhakā antarāpac‍catthikā| katamai tayau? laubhau, bhikkhavai, antarāmalau antarāmalaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) passa itivu॰ 88 antarāamittau antarāsapattau antarāvadhakau antarāpac‍catthikau, dausau, bhikkhavai…pai॰… mauhau, bhikkhavai, antarāmalau antarāamittau antarāsapattau antarāvadhakau antarāpac‍catthikau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau antarāmalā antarāamittā antarāsapattā antarāvadhakā antarāpac‍catthikā’’ti|

anatthajananau laubhau, laubhau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhati||

luddhau atthaṃ na jānāti, luddhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhatamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ laubhau sahatai naraṃ||

anatthajananau dausau, dausau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhati||

duṭṭhau  atthaṃ na jānāti, duṭṭhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhatamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ dausau sahatai naraṃ||

anatthajananau mauhau, mauhau cittappakaupanau|

bhayamantaratau jātaṃ, taṃ janau nāvabujjhati||

mūḷhau  atthaṃ na jānāti, mūḷhau dhammaṃ na passati|

andhatamaṃ tadā hauti, yaṃ mauhau sahatai naranti||

aivampi, tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘tayau khau, mahārāja, purisassa dhammā ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍janti ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya| katamai tayau? laubhau khau, mahārāja, purisassa dhammau ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya; dausau khau, mahārāja…pai॰… mauhau khau, mahārāja, purisassa dhammau ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānau uppaj‍jati ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya| imai khau, mahārāja, tayau purisassa dhammā ajjhattaṃ uppaj‍jamānā uppaj‍janti ahitāya dukkhāya aphāsuvihārāya|

‘‘laubhau dausau ca mauhau ca, purisaṃ pāpacaitasaṃ|

hiṃsanti attasambhūtā, tacasāraṃva samphala’’nti saphalanti (ka॰) passa itivu॰ 50||

aivampi, tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘rāgau ca dausau ca itaunidānā, aratī ratī laumahaṃsau itaujā|

itau samuṭṭhāya manauvitak‍kā, kumārakā dhaṅkamivaussajantī’’ti caṅkamivaussaj‍jantīti (syā॰ ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 273-274||

aivampi, tatrāsayāti – parissayā|

parissayānaṃ sahitāti parissayai sahitā ārādhitā ajjhauttharitā pariyāditā paṭinissatāti – parissayānaṃ sahitā| achambhīti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau  abhīrū acchambhī anutrāsī apalāyī pahīnabhayabhairavau vigatalaumahaṃsau viharatīti – parissayānaṃ sahitā acchambhī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘cātuddisau  appaṭighau ca hauti, santussamānau itarītaraina|

parissayānaṃ sahitā achambhī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

129.

 dussaṅgahā  pabbajitāpi aikai, athau gahaṭṭhā gharamāvasantā|

appaussuk‍kau paraputtaisu hutvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

dussaṅgahā pabbajitāpi aikaiti pabbajitāpi idhaikac‍cai nissayaipi diyyamānai uddaisaipi diyyamānai paripucchāyapi paripucchaipi (ka॰) diyyamānai cīvaraipi diyyamānai pattaipi diyyamānai lauhathālakaipi diyyamānai dhammakaraṇaipi dhammakarakaipi (syā॰) diyyamānai parissāvanaipi diyyamānai thavikaipi diyyamānai upāhanaipi diyyamānai kāyabandhanaipi diyyamānai na suṇanti na sautaṃ audahanti na añ‍ñācittaṃ upaṭṭhapainti, anassavā avacanakarā paṭilaumavuttinau añ‍ñainaiva mukhaṃ karauntīti – dussaṅgahā pabbajitāpi aikai|

athau gahaṭṭhā gharamāvasantāti gahaṭṭhāpi idhaikac‍cai hatthimhipi diyyamānai…pai॰… rathaipi khaittaipi vatthumhipi hirañ‍ñaipi suvaṇṇaipi diyyamānai gāmaipi…pai॰… nigamaipi nagaraipi… raṭṭhaipi… janapadaipi diyyamānai na suṇanti na sautaṃ audahanti na añ‍ñācittaṃ upaṭṭhapainti, anassavā avacanakarā  paṭilaumavuttinau añ‍ñainaiva mukhaṃ karauntīti – athau gahaṭṭhā gharamāvasantā|

appaussuk‍kau paraputtaisu hutvāti attānaṃ ṭhapaitvā sabbai imasmiṃ atthai paraputtā| taisu paraputtaisu appaussuk‍kau hutvā abyāvaṭau hutvā anapaikkhau hutvāti – appaussuk‍kau paraputtaisu hutvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘dussaṅgahā  pabbajitāpi aikai, athau gahaṭṭhā gharamāvasantā|

appaussuk‍kau paraputtaisu hutvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

130.

 auraupayitvā vauraupayitvā (syā॰) gihibyañ‍janāni, sañchin‍napattau saṃsīnapattau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) yathā kauviḷārau|

chaitvāna vīrau gihibandhanāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

auraupayitvā gihibyañ‍janānīti gihibyañ‍janāni vuc‍canti kaisā ca massū ca mālā ca gandhañ‍ca vilaipanañ‍ca ābharaṇañ‍ca pilandhanañ‍ca vatthañ‍ca pārupanañ‍ca vaiṭhanañ‍ca ucchādanaṃ parimaddanaṃ nhāpanaṃ nahāpanaṃ (syā॰) sambāhanaṃ ādāsaṃ añ‍janaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ mukhacuṇṇaṃ  mukhalaipanaṃ hatthabandhaṃ sikhābandhaṃ daṇḍaṃ nāḷikaṃ nālikaṃ (ka॰) passa dī॰ ni॰ 1.16 khaggaṃ chattaṃ citrupāhanaṃ uṇhīsaṃ maṇiṃ vāḷabījaniṃ audātāni vatthāni  dīghadasāni dīgharassāni (syā॰) iti vā| auraupayitvā gihibyañ‍janānīti gihibyañ‍janāni auraupayitvā samauraupayitvā nikkhipitvā paṭipassambhayitvāti – auraupayitvā gihibyañ‍janāni|

sañchin‍napattau yathā kauviḷārauti yathā kauviḷārassa pattāni chin‍nāni sañchin‍nāni patitāni paripatitāni, aivamaiva tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa gihibyañ‍janāni chin‍nāni sañchin‍nāni patitānīti – sañchin‍napattau yathā kauviḷārau|

chaitvāna vīrau gihibandhanānīti| vīrauti vīriyavāti vīrau, pahūti vīrau, visavīti vīrau, alamattauti vīrau, sūrauti vīrau, vik‍kantau abhīrū acchambhī anutrāsī apalāyī pahīnabhayabhairavauti vīrau, vigatalaumahaṃsauti vīrau|

viratau idha āratau idhaiva (syā॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 536 sabbapākaihi, nirayadukkhaṃ atic‍ca vīriyavā sau|

sau vīriyavā padhānavā, dhīrau vīrau (syā॰ ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 536 tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā||

gihibandhanāni  vuc‍canti puttā ca bhariyā ca dāsā ca dāsī ca ajaiḷakā ca kuk‍kuṭasūkarā ca hatthigavāssavaḷavā ca khaittañ‍ca vatthu ca hirañ‍ñañ‍ca suvaṇṇañ‍ca gāmanigamarājadhāniyau ca raṭṭhañ‍ca janapadau ca kausau ca kauṭṭhāgārañ‍ca, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rajanīyavatthu|

chaitvāna vīrau gihibandhanānīti  sau pac‍caikasambuddhau vīrau gihibandhanāni chinditvā samucchinditvā jahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvāti – chaitvāna vīrau gihibandhanāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘auraupayitvā gihibyañ‍janāni, sañchin‍napattau yathā kauviḷārau,

chaitvāna vīrau gihibandhanāni|

aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

paṭhamau vaggau|

dutiyavaggau

131.

 sacai  labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ, saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ|

abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayāni, caraiyya tainattamanau satīmā||

sacai labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyanti sacai nipakaṃ paṇḍitaṃ pañ‍ñavantaṃ buddhimantaṃ ñāṇiṃ vibhāviṃ maidhāviṃ sahāyaṃ labhaiyya paṭilabhaiyya adhigacchaiyya vindaiyyāti – sacai labhaitha nipakaṃ saṃhāyaṃ|

saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīranti| saddhiṃ caranti aikatau caraṃ| sādhuvihārinti paṭhamainapi jhānaina sādhuvihāriṃ, dutiyainapi jhānaina… tatiyainapi jhānaina… catutthainapi jhānaina sādhuvihāriṃ, maittāyapi caitauvimuttiyā sādhuvihāriṃ, karuṇāyapi…pai॰… muditāyapi… upaikkhāyapi caitauvimuttiyā sādhuvihāriṃ, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyāpi sādhuvihāriṃ, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca āyatanasamāpattiyāpi…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyāpi…pai॰… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyāpi  sādhuvihāriṃ, niraudhasamāpattiyāpi sādhuvihāriṃ, phalasamāpattiyāpi sādhuvihāriṃ| dhīranti dhīraṃ paṇḍitaṃ pañ‍ñavantaṃ buddhimantaṃ ñāṇiṃ vibhāviṃ maidhāvinti – saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ|

abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayānīti| parissayāti dvai parissayā – pākaṭaparissayā ca paṭicchan‍naparissayā ca…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti pākaṭaparissayā…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti paṭicchan‍naparissayā…pai॰… aivampi, tatrāsayāti – parissayā| abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayānīti sabbai parissayai abhibhuyya abhibhavitvā ajjhauttharitvā pariyādiyitvā madditvāti – abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayāni|

caraiyya tainattamanau satīmāti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau taina nipakaina paṇḍitaina pañ‍ñavantaina buddhimantaina ñāṇinā vibhāvinā maidhāvinā sahāyaina saddhiṃ attamanau tuṭṭhamanau haṭṭhamanau pahaṭṭhamanau udaggamanau muditamanau caraiyya viharaiyya iriyaiyya vattaiyya pālaiyya yapaiyya yāpaiyyāti – caraiyya tainattamanau| satīmāti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina  saman‍nāgatau, cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritāti – caraiyya tainattamanau satīmā| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘sacai labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ, saddhiṃ  caraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ|

abhibhuyya sabbāni parissayāni, caraiyya tainattamanau satīmā’’ti||

132.

 nau cai labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ, saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ|

rājāva raṭṭhaṃ vijitaṃ pahāya, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

nau cai labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyanti nau cai nipakaṃ paṇḍitaṃ pañ‍ñavantaṃ buddhimantaṃ ñāṇiṃ vibhāviṃ maidhāviṃ sahāyaṃ labhaiyya paṭilabhaiyya adhigacchaiyya vindaiyyāti – nau cai labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ|

saddhiṃ caraṃ  sādhuvihāri dhīranti| saddhiṃ caranti aikatau caraṃ| sādhuvihārinti paṭhamainapi jhānaina sādhuvihāriṃ…pai॰… niraudhasamāpattiyāpi sādhuvihāriṃ, phalasamāpattiyāpi sādhuvihāriṃ| dhīranti dhīraṃ paṇḍitaṃ pañ‍ñavantaṃ buddhimantaṃ ñāṇiṃ vibhāviṃ maidhāvinti – saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ|

rājāva raṭṭhaṃ vijitaṃ pahāyāti rājā khattiyau muddhābhisittau vijitasaṅgāmau nihatapac‍cāmittau laddhādhippāyau paripuṇṇakausakauṭṭhāgārau raṭṭhañ‍ca janapadañ‍ca kausañ‍ca kauṭṭhāgārañ‍ca pahūtahirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaṃ nagarañ‍ca paric‍cajitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā akiñ‍canabhāvaṃ  upagantvā aikau carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti| aivaṃ pac‍caikasambuddhaupi sabbaṃ gharāvāsapalibaudhaṃ chinditvā puttadārapalibaudhaṃ chinditvā ñātipalibaudhaṃ chinditvā mittāmac‍capalibaudhaṃ chinditvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā akiñ‍canabhāvaṃ upagantvā aikau carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaitīti – rājāva raṭṭhaṃ vijitaṃ pahāya, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘nau  cai labhaitha nipakaṃ sahāyaṃ, saddhiṃ caraṃ sādhuvihāri dhīraṃ|

rājāva raṭṭhaṃ vijitaṃ pahāya, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

133.

 addhā pasaṃsāma sahāyasampadaṃ, saiṭṭhā samā saivitabbā sahāyā|

aitai aladdhā anavaj‍jabhaujī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

addhā pasaṃsāma sahāyasampadanti| addhāti aikaṃsavacanaṃ nissaṃsayavacanaṃ nik‍kaṅkhavacanaṃ advaijjhavacanaṃ advaiḷhakavacanaṃ niyaugavacanaṃ apaṇṇakavacanaṃ aviraddhavacanaṃ avatthāpanavacanamaitaṃ – addhāti| sahāyasampadanti sahāyasampadā vuc‍cati yau sau sahāyau asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina  saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina… asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina  … asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina… asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| addhā pasaṃsāma sahāyasampadanti sahāyasampadaṃ pasaṃsāma thaumaima kittaima vaṇṇaimāti – addhā pasaṃsāma sahāyasampadaṃ|

saiṭṭhā samā saivitabbā sahāyāti saiṭṭhā haunti sahāyā sīlaina samādhinā pañ‍ñāya vimuttiyā vimuttiñāṇadassanaina; samā sadisā haunti sahāyā sīlaina samādhinā pañ‍ñāya vimuttiyā vimuttiñāṇadassanaina| saiṭṭhā vā sahāyā sadisā vā sahāyā saivitabbā bhajitabbā payirupāsitabbā paripucchitabbā paripañhitabbāti – saiṭṭhā samā saivitabbā sahāyā|

aitai aladdhā anavaj‍jabhaujīti atthi puggalau sāvaj‍jabhaujī atthi puggalau anavaj‍jabhaujīti| katamau ca puggalau sāvaj‍jabhaujī? idhaikac‍cau puggalau kuhanāya lapanāya naimittikatāya nippaisikatāya lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīsanatāya nijigiṃsanatāya (syā॰) dārudānaina vaiḷudānaina pattadānaina pupphadānaina phaladānaina sinānadānaina cuṇṇadānaina mattikādānaina dantakaṭṭhadānaina mukhaudakadānaina cāṭukamyatāya pātukamyatāya (syā॰) mahāni॰ 159 muggasūpyatāya muggasūpatāya (syā॰) pāribhaṭyatāya pīṭhamaddikatāya vatthuvij‍jāya tiracchānavij‍jāya aṅgavij‍jāya nakkhattavij‍jāya dūtagamanaina pahiṇagamanaina jaṅghapaisaniyaina vaij‍jakammaina navakammaina dūtakammaina (syā॰ ka॰) mahāni॰ 159 piṇḍapaṭipiṇḍakaina dānānuppadānaina, adhammaina visamaina laddhā labhitvā adhigantvā vinditvā paṭilabhitvā jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (ka॰) kappaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati puggalau sāvaj‍jabhaujī|

katamau ca puggalau anavaj‍jabhaujī? idhaikac‍cau puggalau na kuhanāya na lapanāya na naimittikatāya  na nippaisikatāya na lābhaina lābhaṃ nijigīsanatāya na dārudānaina na vaiḷudānaina na pattadānaina na pupphadānaina na phaladānaina na sinānadānaina na cuṇṇadānaina na mattikādānaina na dantakaṭṭhadānaina na mukhaudakadānaina na cāṭukamyatāya na muggasūpyatāya na pāribhaṭyatāya na pīṭhamaddikatāya na vatthuvij‍jāya na tiracchānavij‍jāya na  aṅgavij‍jāya na nakkhattavij‍jāya na dūtagamanaina na pahiṇagamanaina na jaṅghapaisaniyaina na vaij‍jakammaina na navakammaina na piṇḍapaṭipiṇḍakaina na dānānuppadānaina, dhammaina samaina laddhā labhitvā adhigantvā vinditvā paṭilabhitvā jīvikaṃ kappaiti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati puggalau anavaj‍jabhaujī|

aitai aladdhā anavaj‍jabhaujīti aitai anavaj‍jabhaujī aladdhā alabhitvā anadhigantvā avinditvā appaṭilabhitvāti – aitai aladdhā anavaj‍jabhaujī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘addhā pasaṃsāma sahāyasampadaṃ, saiṭṭhā samā saivitabbā sahāyā|

aitai aladdhā anavaj‍jabhaujī, aikau  carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

134.

 disvā suvaṇṇassa pabhassarāni, kammāraputtaina suniṭṭhitāni|

saṅghaṭṭayantāni saṃghaṭṭamānāni (su॰ ni॰ 48) duvai bhujasmiṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

disvā suvaṇṇassa pabhassarānīti disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā| suvaṇṇassāti jātarūpassa| pabhassarānīti parisuddhāni pariyaudātānīti – disvā suvaṇṇassa pabhassarāni|

kammāraputtaina suniṭṭhitānīti kammāraputtau vuc‍cati suvaṇṇakārau| kammāraputtaina suniṭṭhitānīti kammāraputtaina suniṭṭhitāni sukatāni suparikammakatānīti – kammāraputtaina suniṭṭhitāni|

saṅghaṭṭayantāni duvai bhujasminti bhujau vuc‍cati hatthau| yathā aikasmiṃ hatthai dvai nūpurāni dhuvarāni (syā॰) ghaṭṭainti ghaṭainti (syā॰); aivamaiva sattā taṇhāvasaina diṭṭhivasaina nirayai ghaṭṭainti, tiracchānayauniyaṃ ghaṭṭainti, paittivisayai ghaṭṭainti, manussalaukai ghaṭṭainti, daivalaukai ghaṭṭainti, gatiyā gatiṃ upapattiyā  upapattiṃ paṭisandhiyā paṭisandhiṃ bhavaina bhavaṃ saṃsāraina saṃsāraṃ vaṭṭaina vaṭṭaṃ ghaṭṭainti saṅghaṭṭainti saṅghaṭṭaintā  caranti viharanti iriyanti vattainti pālainti yapainti yāpaintīti – saṅghaṭṭayantāni duvai bhujasmiṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘disvā  suvaṇṇassa pabhassarāni, kammāraputtaina suniṭṭhitāni|

saṅghaṭṭayantāni duvai bhujasmiṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’||

135.

 aivaṃ dutīyaina sahā mamassa, vācābhilāpau abhisaj‍janā vā|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ āyatiṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

aivaṃ dutīyaina sahā mamassāti taṇhādutiyau vā hauti puggaladutiyau vā| kathaṃ taṇhādutiyau hauti? taṇhāti rūpataṇhā…pai॰… dhammataṇhā| yassaisā taṇhā appahīnā, sau vuc‍cati taṇhādutiyau|

taṇhādutiyau purisau, dīghamaddhāna saṃsaraṃ|

itthabhāvañ‍ñathābhāvaṃ, saṃsāraṃ nātivattatīti||

aivaṃ taṇhādutiyau vā hauti|

kathaṃ puggaladutiyau hauti? idhaikac‍cau na atthahaitu attahaitu (syā॰) na kāraṇahaitu uddhatau avūpasantacittau aikassa vā dutiyau hauti, dvin‍naṃ vā tatiyau hauti, tiṇṇaṃ vā catutthau hauti| tattha bahuṃ samphappalāpaṃ palapati; saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ mahāmattakathaṃ sainākathaṃ bhayakathaṃ yuddhakathaṃ an‍nakathaṃ pānakathaṃ vatthakathaṃ sayanakathaṃ mālākathaṃ  gandhakathaṃ ñātikathaṃ yānakathaṃ gāmakathaṃ nigamakathaṃ nagarakathaṃ janapadakathaṃ itthikathaṃ itthikathaṃ purisakathaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) dī॰ ni॰ 1.17, 201; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1080 passitabbaṃ sūrakathaṃ visikhākathaṃ kumbhaṭṭhānakathaṃ pubbapaitakathaṃ nānattakathaṃ  laukakkhāyikaṃ samuddakkhāyikaṃ itibhavābhavakathaṃ kathaiti| aivaṃ puggaladutiyau hautīti – aivaṃ dutīyaina sahā mamassa|

vācābhilāpau abhisaj‍janā vāti vācābhilāpau vuc‍cati bāttiṃsa tiracchānakathā, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ…pai॰… itibhavābhavakathaṃ| abhisaj‍janā vāti dvai saj‍janā – taṇhāsaj‍janā ca diṭṭhisaj‍janā ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhāsaj‍janā…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhisaj‍janāti – vācābhilāpau abhisaj‍janā vā|

aitaṃ  bhayaṃ āyatiṃ paikkhamānauti| bhayanti jātibhayaṃ jarābhayaṃ byādhibhayaṃ maraṇabhayaṃ rājabhayaṃ caurabhayaṃ aggibhayaṃ udakabhayaṃ attānuvādabhayaṃ parānuvādabhayaṃ daṇḍabhayaṃ duggatibhayaṃ ūmibhayaṃ kumbhilabhayaṃ āvaṭṭabhayaṃ susumārabhayaṃ ājīvakabhayaṃ asilaukabhayaṃ parisasāraj‍jabhayaṃ madanabhayaṃ bhayānakaṃ chambhitattaṃ laumahaṃsau caitasau ubbaigau utrāsau| aitaṃ bhayaṃ āyatiṃ paikkhamānauti aitaṃ bhayaṃ āyatiṃ paikkhamānau dakkhamānau aulaukayamānau nijjhāyamānau upaparikkhamānauti – aitaṃ bhayaṃ āyatiṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘aivaṃ dutīyaina sahā mamassa, vācābhilāpau abhisaj‍janā vā|

aitaṃ  bhayaṃ āyatiṃ paikkhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’||

136.

 kāmā hi citrā madhurā manauramā, virūparūpaina mathainti cittaṃ|

ādīnavaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

kāmā hi citrā madhurā manauramāti| kāmāti uddānatau dvai kāmā – vatthukāmā ca kilaisakāmā ca…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti vatthukāmā…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti kilaisakāmā| citrāti nānāvaṇṇā rūpā nānāvaṇṇā saddā nānāvaṇṇā gandhā nānāvaṇṇā rasā nānāvaṇṇā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| madhurāti vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ma॰ ni॰ 1.167 passitabbaṃ – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati kāmasukhaṃ miḷhasukhaṃ mīḷhasukhaṃ (passa ma॰ ni॰ 3.328) puthuj‍janasukhaṃ anariyasukhaṃ, na saivitabbaṃ na bhāvaitabbaṃ na bahulīkātabbaṃ, ‘bhāyitabbaṃ aitassa sukhassā’ti vadāmī’’ti – kāmā hi citrā madhurā manauramāti| manauti  yaṃ cittaṃ…pai॰… taj‍jā manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| manau ramainti thaumainti tausainti pahāsaintīti – kāmā hi citrā madhurā manauramā|

virūparūpaina mathainti cittanti nānāvaṇṇaihi rūpaihi…pai॰… nānāvaṇṇaihi phauṭṭhabbaihi cittaṃ mathainti tausainti pahāsaintīti – virūparūpaina mathainti cittaṃ|

ādīnavaṃ  kāmaguṇaisu disvāti| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau? idha, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau yaina sippaṭṭhānaina jīvikaṃ kappaiti, yadi muddāya yadi gaṇanāya yadi saṅkhānaina yadi kasiyā yadi vaṇij‍jāya yadi gaurakkhaina yadi issatthaina issaṭṭhaina (syā॰), issattaina (ka॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.167 yadi rājapaurisaina yadi sippañ‍ñataraina, sītassa purakkhatau uṇhassa purakkhatau ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassaihi samphassamānau rissamānau (ma॰ ni॰ 1.167) khuppipāsāya mīyamānau; ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘tassa cai, bhikkhavai, kulaputtassa aivaṃ uṭṭhahatau ghaṭatau vāyamatau tai bhaugā nābhinipphaj‍janti, sau saucati kilamati paridaivati urattāḷiṃ kandati, sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘maughaṃ vata mai uṭṭhānaṃ, aphalau vata mai vāyāmau’ti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘tassa  cai, bhikkhavai, kulaputtassa aivaṃ uṭṭhahatau ghaṭatau vāyamatau tai bhaugā abhinipphaj‍janti, sau taisaṃ bhaugānaṃ ārakkhādhikaraṇaṃ dukkhaṃ  daumanassaṃ dukkhadaumanassaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) paṭisaṃvaidaiti – ‘kinti mai bhaugai naiva rājānau haraiyyuṃ, na caurā haraiyyuṃ, na aggi ḍahaiyya, na udakaṃ vahaiyya, na appiyā dāyādā haraiyyu’nti| tassa aivaṃ ārakkhatau gaupayatau tai bhaugai rājānau vā haranti caurā vā haranti aggi vā ḍahati udakaṃ vā vahati appiyā vā dāyādā haranti| sau saucati…pai॰… sammauhaṃ āpaj‍jati – ‘yampi mai ahausi tampi nau natthī’ti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu rājānaupi rājūhi vivadanti, khattiyāpi khattiyaihi vivadanti, brāhmaṇāpi brāhmaṇaihi vivadanti, gahapatīpi gahapatīhi vivadanti, mātāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi mātarā vivadati, pitāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi pitarā vivadati, bhātāpi bhaginiyā vivadati, bhaginīpi bhātarā vivadati, sahāyaupi sahāyaina vivadati| tai tattha kalahaviggahavivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pāṇīhipi upak‍kamanti laiḍḍūhipi upak‍kamanti daṇḍaihipi upak‍kamanti satthaihipi upak‍kamanti| tai tattha maraṇampi nigacchanti maraṇamattampi dukkhaṃ| ayampi, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā ubhataubyūḷhaṃ ubhatauviyūḷhaṃ (syā॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.168 saṅgāmaṃ pakkhandanti, usūsupi  khippamānaisu sattīsupi khippamānāsu asīsupi vij‍jautalantaisu| tai tattha usūhipi vijjhanti sattīhipi sattiyāpi (ma॰ ni॰ 1.167, 178) vijjhanti asināpi sīsaṃ chindanti| tai tattha maraṇampi nigacchanti maraṇamattampi dukkhaṃ| ayampi, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu asicammaṃ gahaitvā dhanukalāpaṃ san‍nayhitvā addāvalaipanā addhāvalaipanā (syā॰) upakāriyau  pakkhandanti, usūsupi khippamānaisu sattīsupi khippamānāsu, asīsupi vij‍jautalantaisu| tai tattha usūhipi vijjhanti sattīhipi vijjhanti chakaṇakāyapi chakaṇaṭiyāpi (syā॰), pak‍kaṭṭhiyā (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.168 ausiñ‍canti abhivaggainapi aumaddanti asināpi sīsaṃ chindanti| tai tattha maraṇampi nigacchanti maraṇamattampi dukkhaṃ| ayampi, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau  kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu sandhimpi chindanti nil‍laupampi haranti aikāgārikampi karaunti paripanthaipi tiṭṭhanti paradārampi gacchanti| tamainaṃ rājānau gahaitvā vividhā kammakāraṇā kārainti – kasāhipi tāḷainti, vaittaihipi tāḷainti, aḍḍhadaṇḍakaihipi tāḷainti, hatthampi chindanti…pai॰… asināpi sīsaṃ chindanti| tai tattha maraṇampi nigacchanti maraṇamattampi dukkhaṃ| ayampi, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau sandiṭṭhikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caranti vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caranti manasā duc‍caritaṃ caranti| tai kāyaina duc‍caritaṃ caritvā vācāya duc‍caritaṃ caritvā manasā duc‍caritaṃ caritvā kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍janti| ayampi, bhikkhavai, kāmānaṃ ādīnavau samparāyikau dukkhakkhandhau kāmahaitu kāmanidānaṃ kāmādhikaraṇaṃ kāmānamaiva haitu’’|

ādīnavaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvāti kāmaguṇaisu ādīnavaṃ disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvāti – ādīnavaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘kāmā  hi citrā madhurā manauramā, virūparūpaina mathainti cittaṃ|

ādīnavaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

137.

 ītī  ca gaṇḍau ca upaddavau ca, raugau ca sal‍lañ‍ca bhayañ‍ca maitaṃ|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

ītī ca gaṇḍau ca upaddavau ca, raugau ca sal‍lañ‍ca bhayañ‍ca  maitanti vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘bhayanti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| dukkhanti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| raugauti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| gaṇḍauti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| sal‍lanti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| saṅgauti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| paṅkauti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| gabbhauti, bhikkhavai, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, bhayanti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ? yasmā ca kāmarāgarattāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandarāgavinibaddhau chandarāgavinibandhau (syā॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 8.56 diṭṭhadhammikāpi bhayā na parimuc‍cati, samparāyikāpi bhayā na parimuc‍cati, tasmā bhayanti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| kasmā ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhanti…pai॰… raugauti… gaṇḍauti… sal‍lanti… saṅgauti… paṅkauti… gabbhauti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ? yasmā ca kāmarāgarattāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, chandarāgavinibaddhau diṭṭhadhammikāpi gabbhā na parimuc‍cati, samparāyikāpi gabbhā na parimuc‍cati, tasmā gabbhauti kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacana’’nti|

bhayaṃ dukkhañ‍ca raugau ca, gaṇḍau sal‍lañ‍ca saṅgau ca|

paṅkau gabbhau ca ubhayaṃ, aitai kāmā pavuc‍canti|

yattha sattau puthuj‍janau||

autiṇṇau sātarūpaina, puna gabbhāya gacchati|

yatau ca bhikkhu ātāpī, sampajañ‍ñaṃ na ric‍cati na riñ‍cati (syā॰ ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.251||

sau imaṃ palipathaṃ duggaṃ, atik‍kamma tathāvidhau|

pajaṃ jātijarūpaitaṃ, phandamānaṃ avaikkhatīti||

ītī ca gaṇḍau ca upaddavau ca, raugau ca sal‍lañ‍ca bhayañ‍ca maitaṃ||

aitaṃ  bhayaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvāti aitaṃ bhayaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā passitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā  vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvāti – aitaṃ bhayaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘ītī ca gaṇḍau ca upaddavau ca, raugau ca sal‍lañ‍ca bhayañ‍ca maitaṃ|

aitaṃ bhayaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

138.

 sītañ‍ca uṇhañ‍ca khudaṃ pipāsaṃ, vātātapai ḍaṃsasarīsapai ḍaṃsasiriṃsapai (syā॰), ḍaṃsamakasasarīsapai (ka॰) ca|

sabbānipaitāni abhisambhavitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

sītañ‍ca uṇhañ‍ca khudaṃ pipāsanti| sītanti dvīhi kāraṇaihi sītaṃ hauti – abbhantaradhātuppakaupavasaina vā sītaṃ hauti bahiddhā utuvasaina vā sītaṃ hauti| uṇhanti dvīhi kāraṇaihi uṇhaṃ hauti – abbhantaradhātuppakaupavasaina vā uṇhaṃ hauti bahiddhā utuvasaina vā uṇhaṃ hauti| khudā khuddā (syā॰ ka॰) vuc‍cati chātakau| pipāsā vuc‍cati udakapipāsāti – sītañ‍ca uṇhañ‍ca khudaṃ pipāsaṃ|

vātātapai ḍaṃsasarīsapai cāti| vātāti puratthimā vātā pacchimā vātā uttarā vātā dakkhiṇā vātā sarajā vātā arajā vātā sītā  vātā uṇhā vātā parittā vātā adhimattā vātā vairambhavātā pakkhavātā supaṇṇavātā tālapaṇṇavātā tālavaṇḍavātā (ka॰) cūḷani॰ upasīvamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 43 vidhūpanavātā| ātapau vuc‍cati sūriyasantāpau| ḍaṃsā vuc‍canti piṅgalamakkhikā| sarīsapā vuc‍canti ahīti – vātātapai ḍaṃsasarīsapai ca|

sabbānipaitāni abhisambhavitvāti abhibhavitvā ajjhauttharitvā pariyādiyitvā madditvāti – sabbānipaitāni abhisambhavitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘sītañ‍ca  uṇhañ‍ca khudaṃ pipāsaṃ, vātātapai ḍaṃsasarīsapai ca|

sabbānipaitāni abhisambhavitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

139.

 nāgauva yūthāni vivaj‍jayitvā, sañ‍jātakhandhau padumī uḷārau|

yathābhirantaṃ viharai vīharaṃ (su॰ ni॰ 53) arañ‍ñai, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

nāgauva  yūthāni vivaj‍jayitvāti nāgau vuc‍cati hatthināgau| pac‍caikasambuddhaupi nāgau| kiṃkāraṇā pac‍caikasambuddhau nāgau? āguṃ na karautīti nāgau; na gacchatīti nāgau; na āgacchatīti nāgau| kathaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau āguṃ na karautīti nāgau? āgu vuc‍cati pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṃkilaisikā paunaubhavikā  sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā|

āguṃ na karauti kiñ‍ci laukai, sabbasaṃyaugai visaj‍ja bandhanāni|

sabbattha na saj‍jati vimuttau, nāgau tādi pavuc‍catai tathattā||

aivaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau āguṃ na karautīti nāgau|

kathaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau na gacchatīti nāgau? sau pac‍caikasambuddhau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, na rāgavasaina gacchati, na dausavasaina gacchati, na mauhavasaina gacchati, na mānavasaina gacchati, na diṭṭhivasaina gacchati, na uddhac‍cavasaina gacchati, na vicikicchāvasaina gacchati, na anusayavasaina gacchati, na vaggaihi kappaihi yāyati nīyati niyyati (syā॰ ka॰) vuyhati saṃharīyati| aivaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau na gacchatīti nāgau|

kathaṃ  sau pac‍caikasambuddhau na āgacchatīti nāgau? sautāpattimaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchati, sakadāgāmimaggaina…pai॰… anāgāmimaggaina…pai॰… arahattamaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchati| aivaṃ sau pac‍caikasambuddhau na āgacchatīti nāgau|

nāgauva yūthāni vivaj‍jayitvāti yathā sau hatthināgau yūthāni vivaj‍jaitvā parivaj‍jaitvā abhinivaj‍jaitvā aikauva arañ‍ñavanamajjhaugāhaitvā arañ‍ñai vanamajjhassa ajjhaugāhaitvā (syā॰) carati viharati  iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti, pac‍caikasambuddhaupi gaṇaṃ vivaj‍jaitvā parivaj‍jaitvā abhivaj‍jaitvā aikau aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau (syā॰) arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni  manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni| sau aikau gacchati aikau tiṭṭhati aikau nisīdati aikau saiyyaṃ kappaiti aikau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisati aikau paṭik‍kamati aikau rahau nisīdati aikau caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭhāti aikau carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaitīti – nāgauva yūthāni vivaj‍jayitvā|

sañ‍jātakhandhau padumī uḷārauti yathā sau hatthināgau sañ‍jātakkhandhau sattaratanau vā hauti aṭṭharatanau vā, pac‍caikasambuddhaupi sañ‍jātakkhandhau asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina| yathā sau hatthināgau padumī, pac‍caikasambuddhaupi sattahi baujjhaṅgapupphaihi padumī, satisambaujjhaṅgapupphaina dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgapupphaina vīriyasambaujjhaṅgapupphaina pītisambaujjhaṅgapupphaina, pītisambaujjhaṅgapupphaina passaddhisambaujjhaṅgapupphaina samādhisambaujjhaṅgapupphaina upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgapupphaina| yathā sau hatthināgau uḷārau thāmaina balaina javaina sūraina, pac‍caikasambuddhaupi uḷārau sīlaina samādhinā pañ‍ñāya vimuttiyā vimuttiñāṇadassanaināti – sañ‍jātakhandhau  padumī uḷārau|

yathābhirantaṃ viharai arañ‍ñaiti yathā sau hatthināgau yathābhirantaṃ arañ‍ñai viharati, pac‍caikasambuddhaupi yathābhirantaṃ arañ‍ñai viharati| paṭhamainapi  jhānaina yathābhirantaṃ arañ‍ñai viharati, dutiyainapi jhānaina…pai॰… tatiyainapi jhānaina… catutthainapi jhānaina yathābhirantaṃ arañ‍ñai viharati; maittāyapi caitauvimuttiyā yathābhirantaṃ arañ‍ñai viharati, karuṇāyapi caitauvimuttiyā… muditāyapi caitauvimuttiyā… upaikkhāyapi caitauvimuttiyā yathābhirantaṃ arañ‍ñai viharati; ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyāpi yathābhirantaṃ arañ‍ñai viharati, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasamāpattiyāpi… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyāpi… naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpattiyāpi… niraudhasamāpattiyāpi… phalasamāpattiyāpi yathābhirantaṃ arañ‍ñai viharatīti – yathābhirantaṃ viharai arañ‍ñai, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘nāgauva yūthāni vivaj‍jayitvā, sañ‍jātakhandhau padumī uḷārau|

yathābhirantaṃ viharai arañ‍ñai, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

140.

 aṭṭhānataṃ  saṅgaṇikāratassa, yaṃ phassayai phussayai (syā॰ ka॰) sāmayikaṃ āsāmāyikaṃ (ka॰) vimuttiṃ|

ādic‍cabandhussa vacau nisamma, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

aṭṭhānataṃ  saṅgaṇikāratassa, yaṃ phassayai sāmayikaṃ vimuttinti vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘yāvatānanda sau vatānanda (ma॰ ni॰ 3.186), bhikkhu saṅgaṇikārāmau saṅgaṇikaratau saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ anuyuttau, gaṇārāmau gaṇaratau gaṇasammuditau (gaṇārāmataṃ anuyuttau) ( ) natthi ma॰ ni॰ 3.186, yaṃ taṃ naikkhammasukhaṃ pavivaikasukhaṃ upasamasukhaṃ sambaudhisukhaṃ, tassa sukhassa nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhīti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau ca khau sau, ānanda, bhikkhu aikau gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭhau viharati, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ| yaṃ taṃ naikkhammasukhaṃ pavivaikasukhaṃ upasamasukhaṃ sambaudhisukhaṃ, tassa sukhassa nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhīti – ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| yāvatānanda, bhikkhu saṅgaṇikārāmau saṅgaṇikaratau saṅgaṇikārāmataṃ  anuyuttau, gaṇārāmau gaṇaratau gaṇasammuditau (gaṇārāmataṃ anuyuttau,) sāmāyikaṃ sāmayikaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) vā kantaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissati, asāmāyikaṃ vā akuppanti – naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yau ca khau sau, ānanda, bhikkhu aikau gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭhau viharati, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ sāmāyikaṃ vā kantaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissati, asāmāyikaṃ vā akuppanti, ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jatī’’ti – aṭṭhānataṃ saṅgaṇikāratassa, yaṃ phassayai sāmayikaṃ vimuttiṃ|

ādic‍cabandhussa vacau nisammāti ādic‍cau vuc‍cati sūriyau| sau gautamau gauttaina| pac‍caikasambuddhaupi gautamau gauttaina| sau pac‍caikasambuddhau sūriyassa gauttañātakau gauttabandhu, tasmā  pac‍caikasambuddhau ādic‍cabandhu| ādic‍cabandhussa vacau nisammāti ādic‍cabandhussa vacanaṃ byappathaṃ daisanaṃ anusāsanaṃ anusiṭṭhaṃ sutvā suṇitvā uggahaitvā upadhārayitvā upalakkhayitvāti – ādic‍cabandhussa vacau nisamma, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘aṭṭhānataṃ saṅgaṇikāratassa, yaṃ phassayai sāmayikaṃ vimuttiṃ|

ādic‍cabandhussa vacau nisamma, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

dutiyau vaggau|

tatiyavaggau

141.

 diṭṭhīvisūkāni  upātivattau, pattau niyāmaṃ paṭiladdhamaggau|

uppan‍nañāṇaumhi anañ‍ñanaiyyau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

diṭṭhivisūkāni upātivattauti diṭṭhivisūkāni vuc‍canti vīsativatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhī| idha assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau  ariyadhammai avinītau sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā rūpaṃ rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ, vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ  vā attānaṃ| yā aivarūpā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigataṃ diṭṭhigahanaṃ diṭṭhikantārau diṭṭhivisūkāyikaṃ diṭṭhivipphanditaṃ diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ gāhau paṭiggāhau abhinivaisau parāmāsau kummaggau micchāpathau micchattaṃ titthāyatanaṃ vipariyaisaggāhau viparītaggāhau vipal‍lāsaggāhau micchāgāhau, ayāthāvakasmiṃ yāthāvakanti gāhau, yāvatā dvāsaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni – imāni diṭṭhivisūkāni| diṭṭhivisūkāni upātivattauti diṭṭhivisūkāni upātivattau atik‍kantau samatik‍kantau vītivattauti – diṭṭhīvisūkāni upātivattau|

pattau niyāmaṃ paṭiladdhamaggauti niyāmā vuc‍canti cattārau maggā; ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappau sammāvācā sammākammantau sammāājīvau sammāvāyāmau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| catūhi ariyamaggaihi saman‍nāgatau niyāmaṃ pattau sampattau adhigatau phassitau sacchikatauti – pattau niyāmaṃ| paṭiladdhamaggauti laddhamaggau paṭiladdhamaggau adhigatamaggau phassitamaggau sacchikatamaggauti – pattau niyāmaṃ paṭiladdhamaggau|

uppan‍nañāṇaumhi anañ‍ñanaiyyauti tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ samuppan‍naṃ nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ| ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ samuppan‍naṃ nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtaṃ, ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti… ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti ñāṇaṃ uppan‍naṃ samuppan‍naṃ  nibbattaṃ abhinibbattaṃ pātubhūtanti – uppan‍nañāṇaumhi| anañ‍ñanaiyyauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau na paranaiyyau

na parappattiyau na parappac‍cayau na parapaṭibaddhagū, yathābhūtaṃ taṃ (ka॰) jānāti passati asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau| ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti na paranaiyyau na parappattiyau  na parappac‍cayau na parapaṭibaddhagū, yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatau| ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti na paranaiyyau na  parappattiyau na parappac‍cayau na parapaṭibaddhagū, yathābhūtaṃ jānāti passati asammūḷhau sampajānau paṭissatauti – uppan‍nañāṇaumhi anañ‍ñanaiyyau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘diṭṭhīvisūkāni upātivattau, pattau niyāmaṃ paṭiladdhamaggau|

uppan‍nañāṇaumhi anañ‍ñanaiyyau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

142.

 nil‍laulupau nik‍kuhau nippipāsau, nimmakkhau niddhantakasāvamauhau|

nirāsasau nirāsayau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) su॰ ni॰ 56 sabbalaukai bhavitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

nil‍laulupau nik‍kuhau nippipāsauti lauluppaṃ vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| sā lauluppā taṇhā tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā  anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā pac‍caikasambuddhau nil‍laulupau|

nik‍kuhauti tīṇi kuhanavatthūni – pac‍cayapaṭisaivanasaṅkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu, iriyāpathasaṅkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu, sāmantajappanasaṅkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu|

katamaṃ pac‍cayapaṭisaivanasaṅkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu? idha gahapatikā bhikkhuṃ bhikkhū (ka॰) mahāni॰ 87 nimantainti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi, sau pāpicchau icchāpakatau atthikau cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ bhiyyaukamyataṃ upādāya cīvaraṃ pac‍cakkhāti, piṇḍapātaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, saināsanaṃ pac‍cakkhāti, gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ pac‍cakkhāti| sau aivamāha – ‘‘kiṃ samaṇassa mahagghaina cīvaraina! aitaṃ sāruppaṃ yaṃ samaṇau susānā vā saṅkārakūṭā vā pāpaṇikā vā nantakāni uc‍cinitvā saṅghāṭikaṃ karitvā dhāraiyya| kiṃ samaṇassa mahagghaina piṇḍapātaina! aitaṃ sāruppaṃ yaṃ samaṇau uñchācariyāya piṇḍiyālaupaina jīvikaṃ kappaiyya| kiṃ samaṇassa mahagghaina saināsanaina! aitaṃ sāruppaṃ yaṃ samaṇau  rukkhamūlikau  vā assa sausānikau vā abbhaukāsikau vā| kiṃ samaṇassa mahagghaina gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraina! aitaṃ sāruppaṃ yaṃ samaṇau pūtimuttaina vā haritakīkhaṇḍaina vā ausadhaṃ karaiyyā’’ti| tadupādāya lūkhaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāraiti lūkhaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paribhuñ‍jati lūkhaṃ saināsanaṃ paṭisaivati lūkhaṃ gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paṭisaivati| tamainaṃ gahapatikā  aivaṃ jānanti – ‘‘ayaṃ samaṇau appicchau santuṭṭhau pavivittau asaṃsaṭṭhau āraddhavīriyau dhutavādau’’ti| bhiyyau bhiyyau nimantainti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraihi| sau aivamāha – ‘‘tiṇṇaṃ sammukhībhāvā saddhau kulaputtau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati, saddhāya sammukhībhāvā saddhau kulaputtau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati, daiyyadhammassa sammukhībhāvā saddhau kulaputtau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati, dakkhiṇaiyyānaṃ sammukhībhāvā saddhau kulaputtau bahuṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| tumhākañ‍caivāyaṃ saddhā atthi, daiyyadhammau ca saṃvij‍jati, ahañ‍ca paṭiggāhakau| sacaihaṃ na paṭiggahaissāmi, aivaṃ tumhai puñ‍ñaina paribāhirā bhavissatha bhavissanti (mahāni॰ 87)| na mayhaṃ iminā atthau| api ca, tumhākaṃyaiva anukampāya paṭiggaṇhāmī’’ti| tadupādāya bahumpi cīvaraṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, bahumpi piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, bahumpi saināsanaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, bahumpi gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ paṭiggaṇhāti| yā aivarūpā bhākuṭikā bhākuṭiyaṃ kuhanā kuhāyanā kuhitattaṃ – idaṃ pac‍cayapaṭisaivanasaṅkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu|

katamaṃ iriyāpathasaṅkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu? idhaikac‍cau pāpicchau icchāpakatau sambhāvanādhippāyau ‘‘aivaṃ maṃ janau sambhāvaissatī’’ti gamanaṃ saṇṭhapaiti ṭhānaṃ saṇṭhapaiti nisaj‍jaṃ nisaj‍janaṃ (ka॰) saṇṭhapaiti sayanaṃ saṇṭhapaiti, paṇidhāya gacchati paṇidhāya tiṭṭhati paṇidhāya nisīdati paṇidhāya saiyyaṃ kappaiti samāhitau viya gacchati samāhitau viya  tiṭṭhati samāhitau viya nisīdati samāhitau viya saiyyaṃ kappaiti, āpāthakajjhāyīva āpātakajjhāyī ca (ka॰) hauti| yā aivarūpā iriyāpathassa āṭhapanā ṭhapanā saṇṭhapanā bhākuṭikā bhākuṭiyaṃ kuhanā kuhāyanā kuhitattaṃ kuhāyitattaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), visuddhimaggaṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā – idaṃ iriyāpathasaṅkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu|

katamaṃ  sāmantajappanasaṅkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu? idhaikac‍cau pāpicchau icchāpakatau sambhāvanādhippāyau ‘‘aivaṃ maṃ janau sambhāvaissatī’’ti ariyadhammai san‍nissitavācaṃ bhāsati| ‘‘yau aivarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāraiti sau samaṇau mahaisakkhau’’ti bhaṇati; yau aivarūpaṃ pattaṃ dhāraiti…pai॰… lauhathālakaṃ dhāraiti… dhammakaraṇaṃ dhāraiti… parissāvanaṃ dhāraiti… kuñ‍cikaṃ dhāraiti… upāhanaṃ dhāraiti… kāyabandhanaṃ dhāraiti… āyaugaṃ āyaugabandhanaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) mahāni॰ 87 dhāraiti sau samaṇau mahaisakkhau’’ti bhaṇati; ‘‘yassa aivarūpā upajjhāyau sau samaṇau mahaisakkhau’’ti bhaṇati; yassa aivarūpau ācariyau  …pai॰… aivarūpā samānupajjhāyakā… samānācariyakā… mittā… sandiṭṭhā… sambhattā… sahāyā sau samaṇau mahaisakkhauti bhaṇati; yau aivarūpai vihārai vasati… aḍḍhayaugai vasati… pāsādai vasati… hammiyai vasati… guhāyaṃ vasati… laiṇai vasati… kuṭiyaṃ vasati… kūṭāgārai vasati… aṭṭai vasati… māḷai vasati… uddaṇḍai uṭṭaṇḍai (ka॰) vasati… upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ vasati… maṇḍapai vasati… rukkhamūlai vasati sau samaṇau mahaisakkhau’’ti bhaṇati|

atha vā, kaurajikakaurajikau bhākuṭikabhākuṭikau kuhakakuhakau lapakalapakau mukhasambhāvikau ‘‘ayaṃ samaṇau imāsaṃ  aivarūpānaṃ santānaṃ vihārasamāpattīnaṃ lābhī’’ti tādisaṃ gambhīraṃ gūḷhaṃ nipuṇaṃ paṭicchan‍naṃ laukuttaraṃ suñ‍ñatāpaṭisañ‍ñuttaṃ kathaṃ kathaiti| yā aivarūpā bhākuṭikā bhākuṭiyaṃ kuhanā kuhāyanā kuhitattaṃ, idaṃ sāmantajappanasaṅkhātaṃ kuhanavatthu| tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa imāni tīṇi kuhanavatthūni pahīnāni samucchin‍nāni vūpasantāni paṭippassaddhāni abhabbuppattikāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni| tasmā sau pac‍caikasambuddhau nik‍kuhau|

nippipāsauti pipāsā vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| sā pipāsā taṇhā tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā pac‍caikasambuddhau nippipāsauti – nil‍laulupau nik‍kuhau nippipāsau|
