nimmakkhau  niddhantakasāvamauhauti| makkhauti yau makkhau makkhāyanā makkhiyanā (ka॰) passa vibha॰ 892 makkhāyitattaṃ niṭṭhuriyaṃ niṭṭhuriyakammaṃ| kasāvauti rāgau kasāvau, dausau kasāvau, mauhau kasāvau, kaudhau…pai॰… upanāhau… makkhau… paḷāsau… sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārā kasāvā| mauhauti dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhasamudayai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇaṃ, pubbantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, aparantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, pubbantāparantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ, idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu dhammaisu añ‍ñāṇaṃ| yaṃ aivarūpaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ anabhisamayau ananubaudhau appaṭivaidhau  asaṃgāhanā apariyaugāhanā asamapaikkhanā apac‍cavaikkhaṇā apac‍cakkhakammaṃ dummaijjhaṃ bālyaṃ asampajañ‍ñaṃ mauhau pamauhau sammauhau avij‍jā avij‍jaughau avij‍jāyaugau avij‍jānusayau avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa makkhau ca kasāvau ca mauhau ca vantā saṃvantā niddhantā  pahīnā samucchin‍nā vūpasantā paṭippassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāti| sau pac‍caikasambuddhau nimmakkhau niddhantakasāvamauhau|

nirāsasau sabbalaukai bhavitvāti āsā vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| sabbalaukaiti sabbaapāyalaukai sabbamanussalaukai sabbadaivalaukai sabbakhandhalaukai sabbadhātulaukai sabbaāyatanalaukai| nirāsasau sabbalaukai bhavitvāti sabbalaukai nirāsasau bhavitvā nittaṇhau bhavitvā nippipāsau bhavitvāti – nirāsasau sabbalaukai bhavitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘nil‍laulupau nik‍kuhau nippipāsau, nimmakkhau niddhantakasāvamauhau|

nirāsasau sabbalaukai bhavitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

143.

 pāpaṃ sahāyaṃ parivaj‍jayaitha, anatthadassiṃ  visamai niviṭṭhaṃ|

sayaṃ na saivai pasutaṃ pamattaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

pāpaṃ  sahāyaṃ parivaj‍jayaithāti| pāpasahāyau vuc‍cati yau sau sahāyau dasavatthukāya micchādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau – natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau, natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā, natthi laukai samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintīti| ayaṃ pāpasahāyau| pāpaṃ sahāyaṃ parivaj‍jayaithāti| pāpaṃ sahāyaṃ vaj‍jaiyya parivaj‍jaiyyāti – pāpaṃ sahāyaṃ parivaj‍jayaitha|

anatthadassiṃ visamai niviṭṭhanti anatthadassī vuc‍cati yau sau sahāyau dasavatthukāya micchādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatau – natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidaintīti| visamai niviṭṭhanti visamai kāyakammai niviṭṭhaṃ, visamai vacīkammai niviṭṭhaṃ, visamai manaukammai niviṭṭhaṃ, visamai pāṇātipātai niviṭṭhaṃ, visamai adin‍nādānai niviṭṭhaṃ, visamai kāmaisumicchācārai niviṭṭhaṃ, visamai musāvādai niviṭṭhaṃ, visamāya pisuṇāya vācāya pisuṇavācāya (ka॰) niviṭṭhaṃ, visamāya pharusāya vācāya pharusavācāya (ka॰) niviṭṭhaṃ, visamai samphappalāpai niviṭṭhaṃ, visamāya abhijjhāya niviṭṭhaṃ, visamai byāpādai niviṭṭhaṃ, visamāya micchādiṭṭhiyā niviṭṭhaṃ, visamaisu saṅkhāraisu  niviṭṭhaṃ visamaisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu niviṭṭhaṃ, visamaisu pañ‍casu nīvaraṇaisu niviṭṭhaṃ  viniviṭṭhaṃ sattaṃ al‍līnaṃ upagataṃ ajjhausitaṃ adhimuttanti – anatthadassiṃ visamai niviṭṭhaṃ|

sayaṃ na saivai pasutaṃ pamattanti| pasutanti yaupi kāmai aisati gavaisati pariyaisati tac‍caritau tabbahulau taggarukau tan‍ninau tappauṇau tappabbhārau tadadhimuttau tadadhipataiyyau, saupi kāmappasutau| yaupi taṇhāvasaina rūpai pariyaisati, saddai…pai॰… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai pariyaisati tac‍caritau tabbahulau taggarukau tan‍nin‍nau tappauṇau tappabbhārau tadadhimuttau tadadhipataiyyau, saupi kāmappasutau| yaupi taṇhāvasaina rūpai paṭilabhati, saddai…pai॰… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai paṭilabhati tac‍caritau tabbahulau taggarukau tan‍nin‍nau tappauṇau tappabbhārau tadadhimuttau tadadhipataiyyau, saupi kāmappasutau| yaupi taṇhāvasaina rūpai paribhuñ‍jati, saddai…pai॰… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai paribhuñ‍jati tac‍caritau tabbahulau taggarukau tan‍nin‍nau tappauṇau tappabbhārau tadadhimuttau tadadhipataiyyau, saupi kāmappasutau| yathā kalahakārakau kalahappasutau, kammakārakau kammappasutau, gaucarai carantau gaucarappasutau, jhāyī jhānappasutau; aivamaiva yau kāmai aisati gavaisati pariyaisati tac‍caritau tabbahulau taggarukau tan‍nin‍nau tappauṇau tappabbhārau tadadhimuttau tadadhipataiyyau, saupi kāmappasutau| yaupi taṇhāvasaina rūpai pariyaisati…pai॰… yaupi taṇhāvasaina rūpai paṭilabhati…pai॰… yaupi taṇhāvasaina rūpai paribhuñ‍jati, saddai…pai॰… gandhai… rasai… phauṭṭhabbai  paribhuñ‍jati tac‍caritau tabbahulau taggarukau tan‍nin‍nau tappauṇau tappabbhārau tadadhimuttau tadadhipataiyyau, saupi kāmappasutau| pamattanti| pamādau vattabbau kāyaduc‍caritai vā vacīduc‍caritai vā manauduc‍caritai vā pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu vā cittassa vausaggau vausaggānuppadānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ bhāvanāya asak‍kac‍cakiriyatā asātac‍cakiriyatā anaṭṭhitakiriyatā aulīnavuttitā nikkhittacchandatā nikkhittadhuratā anāsaivanā abhāvanā abahulīkammaṃ anadhiṭṭhānaṃ ananuyaugau, yau aivarūpau pamādau pamaj‍janā pamaj‍jitattaṃ – ayaṃ vuc‍cati pamādau|

sayaṃ na saivai pasutaṃ pamattanti pasutaṃ na saivaiyya pamattañ‍ca sayaṃ na saivaiyya sāmaṃ na saivaiyya na nisaivaiyya na saṃsaivaiyya na parisaṃsaivaiyya na ācaraiyya na samācaraiyya na samādāya vattaiyyāti  – sayaṃ na saivai pasutaṃ pamattaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘pāpaṃ  sahāyaṃ parivaj‍jayaitha, anatthadassiṃ visamai niviṭṭhaṃ|

sayaṃ na saivai pasutaṃ pamattaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

144.

 bahussutaṃ dhammadharaṃ bhajaitha, mittaṃ uḷāraṃ paṭibhānavantaṃ|

añ‍ñāya atthāni vinaiyya kaṅkhaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

bahussutaṃ  dhammadharaṃ bhajaithāti bahussutau hauti mittau sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ satthā sabyañ‍janā (syā॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 3.82 kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā dhatā (syā॰) vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| dhammadharanti dhammaṃ dhāraintaṃ dhāraiti (ka॰) – suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthaṃ udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| bahussutaṃ dhammadharaṃ bhajaithāti bahussutañ‍ca dhammadharañ‍ca mittaṃ bhajaiyya saṃbhajaiyya saivaiyya nisaivaiyya saṃsaivaiyya paṭisaivaiyyāti – bahussutaṃ dhammadharaṃ bhajaitha|

mittaṃ uḷāraṃ paṭibhānavantanti uḷārau hauti mittau sīlaina  samādhinā pañ‍ñāya vimuttiyā vimuttiñāṇadassaina| paṭibhānavantanti tayau paṭibhānavantau – pariyattipaṭibhānavā, paripucchāpaṭibhānavā, adhigamapaṭibhānavā| katamau pariyattipaṭibhānavā? idhaikac‍cassa buddhavacanaṃ pariyāputaṃ pariyāpuṭaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) passa dī॰ ni॰ 3.282 hauti suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ| tassa pariyattiṃ nissāya paṭibhāti – ayaṃ pariyattipaṭibhānavā|

katamau paripucchāpaṭibhānavā? idhaikac‍cau paripucchitaupi hauti atthai ca ñāyai ca lakkhaṇai ca kāraṇai ca ṭhānāṭṭhānai ca| tassa paripucchaṃ nissāya paṭibhāti – ayaṃ paripucchāpaṭibhānavā|

katamau adhigamapaṭibhānavā? idhaikac‍cassa adhigatā haunti cattārau satipaṭṭhānā cattārau sammappadhānā cattārau iddhipādā pañ‍cindriyāni pañ‍ca balāni satta baujjhaṅgā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau cattārau ariyamaggā cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalāni catassau paṭisambhidāyau cha abhiñ‍ñāyau chaḷabhiñ‍ñāyau (syā॰)| tassa atthau ñātau dhammau ñātau nirutti ñātā| atthai ñātai atthau paṭibhāti, dhammai ñātai dhammau paṭibhāti, niruttiyā ñātāya nirutti paṭibhāti| imaisu tīsu ñāṇaṃ paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| sau pac‍caikasambuddhau imāya paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāya upaitau samupaitau upāgatau  samupāgatau upapan‍nau samupapan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| tasmā pac‍caikasambuddhau  paṭibhānavā| yassa pariyatti natthi paripucchā natthi adhigamau natthi, kiṃ tassa paṭibhāyissatīti – mittaṃ uḷāraṃ paṭibhānavantaṃ|

añ‍ñāya atthāni vinaiyya kaṅkhanti attatthaṃ añ‍ñāya paratthaṃ añ‍ñāya ubhayatthaṃ añ‍ñāya diṭṭhadhammikatthaṃ añ‍ñāya samparāyikatthaṃ añ‍ñāya paramatthaṃ añ‍ñāya abhiñ‍ñāya jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvā kaṅkhaṃ vinaiyya paṭivinaiyya pajahaiyya vinaudaiyya byantīkaraiyya anabhāvaṃ gamaiyyāti – añ‍ñāya atthāni vinaiyya kaṅkhaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘bahussutaṃ dhammadharaṃ bhajaitha, mittaṃ uḷāraṃ paṭibhānavantaṃ|

añ‍ñāya atthāni vinaiyya kaṅkhaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

145.

 khiḍḍaṃ ratiṃ ratī (syā॰) kāmasukhañ‍ca laukai, analaṅkaritvā anapaikkhamānau|

vibhūsaṭṭhānā vibhūsanaṭṭhānā (syā॰ ka॰), vibhūsaṇaṭṭhānā (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) viratau sac‍cavādī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

khiḍḍaṃ ratiṃ kāmasukhañ‍ca laukaiti| khiḍḍāti dvai khiḍḍā – kāyikā khiḍḍā ca vācasikā khiḍḍā ca…pai॰… ayaṃ kāyikā  khiḍḍā…pai॰… ayaṃ vācasikā khiḍḍā| ratīti anuk‍kaṇṭhitādhivacanamaitaṃ – ratīti| kāmasukhanti vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā passa ma॰ ni॰ 2.280 – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati kāmasukhaṃ’’| laukaiti manussalaukaiti – khiḍḍaṃ ratiṃ kāmasukhañ‍ca laukai|

analaṅkaritvā  anapaikkhamānauti khiḍḍañ‍ca ratiñ‍ca kāmasukhañ‍ca laukai analaṅkaritvā anapaikkhau hutvā pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvāti – analaṅkaritvā anapaikkhamānau|

vibhūsaṭṭhānā  viratau sac‍cavādīti| vibhūsāti dvai vibhūsā – atthi agārikavibhūsā agārikassa vibhūsā (ka॰) aivamuparipi atthi anāgārikavibhūsā| katamā agārikavibhūsā? kaisā ca massū ca mālāgandhañ‍ca vilaipanañ‍ca ābharaṇañ‍ca pilandhanañ‍ca vatthañ‍ca pārupanañ‍ca pasādhanañ‍ca (syā॰), sayanāsanañ‍ca (ka॰) cūḷani॰ khaggavisāṇasuttaniddaisa 130 natthi pāṭhanānattaṃ vaiṭhanañ‍ca ucchādanaṃ parimaddanaṃ nhāpanaṃ sambāhanaṃ ādāsaṃ añ‍janaṃ mālāgandhavilaipanaṃ mukhacuṇṇaṃ mukhalaipanaṃ hatthabandhaṃ sikhābandhaṃ  daṇḍaṃ nāḷikaṃ khaggaṃ chattaṃ citrupāhanaṃ uṇhīsaṃ maṇiṃ vāḷabījaniṃ audātāni vatthāni dīghadasāni iti vā – ayaṃ agārikavibhūsā|

katamā anāgārikavibhūsā? cīvaramaṇḍanā pattamaṇḍanā saināsanamaṇḍanā imassa vā pūtikāyassa bāhirānaṃ vā parikkhārānaṃ maṇḍanā vibhūsanā kaiḷanā parikaiḷanā gaddhikatā gaddhikattaṃ gaidhikatā gaidhikattaṃ (syā॰) passa vibha॰ 854 capalatā capalanā (syā॰) cāpalyaṃ – ayaṃ anāgārikavibhūsā|

sac‍cavādīti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau sac‍cavādī sac‍casandhau thaitau pac‍cayikau avisaṃvādakau laukassa, vibhūsaṭṭhānā āratau viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau, vimariyādikataina caitasā viharatīti – vibhūsaṭṭhānā viratau sac‍cavādī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘khiḍḍaṃ ratiṃ kāmasukhañ‍ca laukai, analaṅkaritvā anapaikkhamānau|

vibhūsaṭṭhānā viratau sac‍cavādī, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

146.

 puttañ‍ca dāraṃ pitarañ‍ca mātaraṃ, dhanāni dhañ‍ñāni ca bandhavāni|

hitvāna kāmāni yathaudhikāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

puttañ‍ca  dāraṃ pitarañ‍ca mātaranti| puttāti cattārau puttā  – atrajau puttau, khaittajau khaitrajau (syā॰ ka॰) puttau, din‍nakau puttau, antaivāsikau puttau| dārā vuc‍canti bhariyāyau| pitāti yau sau janakau| mātāti yā sā janikāti – puttañ‍ca dāraṃ pitarañ‍ca mātaraṃ|

dhanāni dhañ‍ñāni ca bandhavānīti dhanāni vuc‍canti hirañ‍ñaṃ suvaṇṇaṃ muttā maṇi vaiḷuriyau saṅkhau silā pavāḷaṃ rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ lauhitaṅgau lauhitakau (?) masāragal‍laṃ| dhañ‍ñāni vuc‍canti pubbaṇṇaṃ aparaṇṇaṃ| pubbaṇṇaṃ nāma sāli vīhi yavau gaudhumau kaṅgu varakau kudrūsakau kudrusakau (syā॰)| aparaṇṇaṃ  nāma sūpaiyyaṃ| bandhavānīti cattārau bandhavā – ñātibandhavāpi bandhu, gauttabandhavāpi bandhu, mittabandhavāpi bandhu, sippabandhavāpi bandhūti – dhanāni dhañ‍ñāni ca bandhavāni|

hitvāna kāmāni yathaudhikānīti| kāmāti uddānatau dvai kāmā – vatthukāmā ca kilaisakāmā ca…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti vatthukāmā…pai॰… imai vuc‍canti kilaisakāmā| hitvāna kāmānīti vatthukāmai parijānitvā, kilaisakāmai pahāya pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvā| hitvāna kāmāni yathaudhikānīti sautāpattimaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā, tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchati; sakadāgāmimaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā…pai॰… anāgāmimaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā… arahattamaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā, tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchatīti – hitvāna  kāmāni yathaudhikāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘puttañ‍ca dāraṃ pitarañ‍ca mātaraṃ, dhanāni dhañ‍ñāni ca bandhavāni|

hitvāna kāmāni yathaudhikāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

147.

 saṅgau  aisau parittamaittha saukhyaṃ, appassādau dukkhamaittha bhiyyau|

gaḷau aisau iti ñatvā matimā mutīmā (su॰ ni॰ 61), aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

saṅgau aisau parittamaittha saukhyanti saṅgauti vā baḷisanti vā āmisanti vā laggananti vā palibaudhauti vā, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| parittamaittha saukhyanti vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā…pai॰… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati kāmasukhaṃ| appakaṃ aitaṃ sukhaṃ, parittakaṃ aitaṃ sukhaṃ, thaukakaṃ aitaṃ sukhaṃ, aumakaṃ aitaṃ sukhaṃ, lāmakaṃ aitaṃ sukhaṃ, chatuk‍kaṃ aitaṃ sukha’’nti – saṅgau aisau parittamaittha saukhyaṃ|

appassādau dukkhamaittha bhiyyauti appassādā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.237 bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā bahūpāyāsā (syā॰); ādīnavau aittha bhiyyau| aṭṭhikaṅkalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, maṃsapaisūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, tiṇuk‍kūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, aṅgārakāsūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, supinakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, yācitakūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, rukkhaphalūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, asisūnūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, sattisūlūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā, sappasirūpamā kāmā vuttā bhagavatā bahudukkhā bahupāyāsā, ādīnavau aittha bhiyyauti – appassādau dukkhamaittha bhiyyau|

gaḷau aisau iti ñatvā matimāti gaḷauti vā baḷisanti vā āmisanti vā laggananti vā bandhananti vā palibaudhauti vā, pañ‍can‍naitaṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ adhivacanaṃ| itīti padasandhi padasaṃsaggau padapāripūrī akkharasamavāyau byañ‍janasiliṭṭhatā padānupubbatāpaitaṃ itīti| matimāti paṇḍitau pañ‍ñavā buddhimā ñāṇī vibhāvī maidhāvī| gaḷau aisau iti ñatvā matimāti matimā gaḷauti ñatvā baḷisanti ñatvā āmisaṃ ti ñatvā  laggananti ñatvā bandhananti ñatvā palibaudhauti ñatvā jānitvā tulayitvā tīrayitvā vibhāvayitvā vibhūtaṃ katvāti – gaḷau  aisau iti ñatvā matimā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘saṅgau aisau parittamaittha saukhyaṃ, appassādau dukkhamaittha bhiyyau|

gaḷau aisau iti ñatvā matimā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

148.

 sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanāni, jālaṃva bhaitvā salilambucārī|

aggīva daḍḍhaṃ anivattamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanānīti dasa saṃyaujanāni – kāmarāgasaṃyaujanaṃ, paṭighasaṃyaujanaṃ, mānasaṃyaujanaṃ, diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaṃ, vicikicchāsaṃyaujanaṃ, sīlabbataparāmāsasaṃyaujanaṃ, bhavarāgasaṃyaujanaṃ, issāsaṃyaujanaṃ, macchariyasaṃyaujanaṃ, avij‍jāsaṃyaujanaṃ| sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanānīti dasa saṃyaujanāni dālayitvā sandālayitvā pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvāti – sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanāni|

jālaṃva bhaitvā salilambucārīti jālaṃ vuc‍cati suttajālaṃ| salilaṃ vuc‍cati udakaṃ| ambucārī vuc‍cati macchau| yathā macchau jālaṃ bhinditvā pabhinditvā dālayitvā padālayitvā sampadālayitvā carati viharati  iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti, aivamaiva dvai jālā – taṇhājālañ‍ca diṭṭhijālañ‍ca…pai॰… idaṃ taṇhājālaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ diṭṭhijālaṃ| tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa  taṇhājālaṃ pahīnaṃ, diṭṭhijālaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ| taṇhājālassa pahīnattā diṭṭhijālassa paṭinissaṭṭhattā sau pac‍caikasambuddhau rūpai na saj‍jati saddai na saj‍jati gandhai na saj‍jati…pai॰… diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu na saj‍jati na gaṇhāti na bajjhati na palibajjhati, nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharatīti – jālaṃva bhaitvā salilambucārī|

aggīva  daḍḍhaṃ anivattamānauti yathā aggi tiṇakaṭṭhupādānaṃ dahantau gacchati anivattantau, aivamaiva tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa sautāpattimaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchati, sakadāgāmimaggaina…pai॰… anāgāmimaggaina… arahattamaggaina yai kilaisā pahīnā tai kilaisai na punaiti na pac‍caiti na pac‍cāgacchatīti – aggīva daḍḍhaṃ anivattamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanāni, jālaṃva bhaitvā salilambucārī|

aggīva daḍḍhaṃ anivattamānau, aikau  carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

149.

 aukkhittacakkhu na ca pādalaulau, guttindriyau rakkhitamānasānau|

anavassutau apariḍayhamānau aparidayhamānau (ka॰), aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

aukkhittacakkhu na ca pādalaulauti kathaṃ khittacakkhu hauti? idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu natthi syā॰ pautthakai mahāni॰ 157 cakkhulaulau cakkhulauliyaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| adiṭṭhaṃ dakkhitabbaṃ diṭṭhaṃ samatik‍kamitabbanti – ārāmaina ārāmaṃ uyyānaina uyyānaṃ gāmaina gāmaṃ nigamaina nigamaṃ nagaraina nagaraṃ raṭṭhaina raṭṭhaṃ janapadaina janapadaṃ dīghacārikaṃ anavaṭṭhitacārikaṃ anavatthacārikaṃ (syā॰) anuyuttau hauti rūpadassanāya| aivaṃ khittacakkhu hauti|

atha vā, bhikkhu antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau vīthiṃ paṭipan‍nau asaṃvutau gacchati| hatthiṃ aulaukaintau assaṃ aulaukaintau rathaṃ aulaukaintau pattiṃ aulaukaintau kumārakai aulaukaintau kumārikāyau aulaukaintau itthiyau aulaukaintau purisai aulaukaintau antarāpaṇaṃ aulaukaintau gharamukhāni aulaukaintau uddhaṃ aulaukaintau adhau aulaukaintau disāvidisaṃ vipaikkhamānau paikkhamānau (syā॰ ka॰) gacchati| aivampi khittacakkhu hauti|

atha  vā, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā nimittaggāhī hauti anubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya na paṭipaj‍jati, na  rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai na saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivampi khittacakkhu hauti|

yathā vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ visūkadassanaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – nac‍caṃ gītaṃ vāditaṃ paikkhaṃ akkhānaṃ pāṇissaraṃ vaitāḷaṃ kumbhathūṇaṃ saubhanakaṃ saubhanagarakaṃ (syā॰), saubhanakaraṇaṃ (ka॰) caṇḍālaṃ vaṃsaṃ dhauvanaṃ hatthiyuddhaṃ assayuddhaṃ mahiṃsayuddhaṃ mahisayuddhaṃ (syā॰) usabhayuddhaṃ ajayuddhaṃ maiṇḍayuddhaṃ kuk‍kuṭayuddhaṃ vaṭṭakayuddhaṃ daṇḍayuddhaṃ muṭṭhiyuddhaṃ nibbuddhaṃ uyyaudhikaṃ balaggaṃ sainābyūhaṃ anīkadassanaṃ iti vā| iti aivarūpaṃ visūkadassanaṃ anuyuttau hauti| aivampi khittacakkhu hauti|

kathaṃ aukkhittacakkhu hauti? idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu na cakkhulaulau na cakkhulauliyaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| adiṭṭhaṃ dakkhitabbaṃ diṭṭhaṃ samatik‍kamitabbanti – na ārāmaina ārāmaṃ na uyyānaina uyyānaṃ na gāmaina gāmaṃ na nigamaina nigamaṃ na nagaraina nagaraṃ na raṭṭhaina raṭṭhaṃ na janapadaina janapadaṃ dīghacārikaṃ anavaṭṭhitacārikaṃ anuyuttau hauti rūpadassanāya| aivaṃ aukkhittacakkhu hauti|

atha vā, bhikkhu antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau vīthiṃ paṭipan‍nau saṃvutau gacchati| na hatthiṃ aulaukaintau na assaṃ aulaukaintau na rathaṃ aulaukaintau na pattiṃ aulaukaintau na kumārakai aulaukaintau na kumārikāyau aulaukaintau na itthiyau aulaukaintau na purisai aulaukaintau na antarāpaṇaṃ aulaukaintau na gharamukhāni aulaukaintau na uddhaṃ aulaukaintau na  adhau aulaukaintau na disāvidisaṃ vipaikkhamānau gacchati| aivampi aukkhittacakkhu hauti|

atha vā, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| aivampi aukkhittacakkhu hauti|

yathā  vā panaikai bhauntau samaṇabrāhmaṇā saddhādaiyyāni bhaujanāni bhuñ‍jitvā tai aivarūpaṃ visūkadassanaṃ anuyuttā viharanti, saiyyathidaṃ – nac‍caṃ gītaṃ vāditaṃ…pai॰… anīkadassanaṃ iti vā| iti aivarūpā visūkadassanā paṭiviratau| aivampi aukkhittacakkhu hauti|

na ca pādalaulauti  kathaṃ pādalaulau hauti? idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu pādalaulau pādalauliyaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ārāmaina ārāmaṃ uyyānaina uyyānaṃ gāmaina gāmaṃ nigamaina nigamaṃ nagaraina nagaraṃ raṭṭhaina raṭṭhaṃ janapadaina janapadaṃ dīghacārikaṃ anavaṭṭhitacārikaṃ anuyuttau hauti| aivampi pādalaulau hauti|

atha vā, bhikkhu antausaṅghārāmai antaupi saṃghārāmai (ka॰) pādalaulau pādalauliyaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na atthahaitu na kāraṇahaitu uddhatau avūpasantacittau parivaiṇatau parivaiṇaṃ gacchati, vihāratau vihāraṃ gacchati, aḍḍhayaugatau aḍḍhayaugaṃ gacchati, pāsādatau pāsādaṃ gacchati, hammiyatau hammiyaṃ gacchati, guhatau guhaṃ gacchati, laiṇatau laiṇaṃ gacchati, kuṭiyā kuṭiṃ gacchati, kūṭāgāratau kūṭāgāraṃ gacchati, aṭṭatau  aṭṭaṃ gacchati, māḷatau māḷaṃ gacchati, uddaṇḍatau uddaṇḍaṃ gacchati uddaṇḍaṃ gacchati, uddhausitatau uddhausitaṃ gacchati (syā॰) passa mahāni॰ 17, upaṭṭhānasālatau upaṭṭhānasālaṃ gacchati, maṇḍapatau maṇḍapaṃ gacchati, rukkhamūlatau rukkhamūlaṃ gacchati, yattha vā pana bhikkhū nisīdanti vā gacchanti vā, tattha aikassa vā dutiyau hauti, dvin‍naṃ vā tatiyau hauti, tiṇṇaṃ vā catutthau hauti| tattha bahuṃ samphappalāpaṃ palapati, saiyyathidaṃ – rājakathaṃ caurakathaṃ…pai॰… iti bhavābhavakathaṃ kathaiti| aivampi pādalaulau hauti|

na ca pādalaulauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau pādalauliyā āratau viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā paṭisal‍lānārāmau hauti paṭisal‍lānaratau ajjhattaṃ caitausamathamanuyuttau anirākatajjhānau vipassanāya saman‍nāgatau brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgāraṃ jhāyī jhānaratau aikattamanuyuttau sadatthagarukauti – aukkhittacakkhu na ca pādalaulau|

guttindriyau  rakkhitamānasānauti| guttindriyauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati cakkhundriyaṃ, cakkhundriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jati| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… ghānaina gandhaṃ ghāyitvā… jivhāya rasaṃ sāyitvā… kāyaina phauṭṭhabbaṃ phusitvā … manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya na nimittaggāhī hauti nānubyañ‍janaggāhī| yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ manindriyaṃ asaṃvutaṃ viharantaṃ abhijjhādaumanassā pāpakā akusalā dhammā anvāssavaiyyuṃ, tassa saṃvarāya paṭipaj‍jati, rakkhati manindriyaṃ, manindriyai saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatīti – guttindriyau| rakkhitamānasānauti gaupitamānasānauti – guttindriyau rakkhitamānasānau|

anavassutau pariḍayhamānauti vuttañhaitaṃ āyasmatā mahāmauggal‍lānaina – ‘‘avassutapariyāyañ‍ca passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.243 vau, āvusau, daisaissāmi anavassutapariyāyañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasikarautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau mahāmauggal‍lānassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā mahāmauggal‍lānau mahāmauggalānau (ka॰) aitadavauca –

‘‘kathaṃ cāvusau, avassutau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati anupaṭṭhitakāyasati (syā॰ ka॰) ca viharati parittacaitasau| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, yatthassa tattha yai (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.243 tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā  nirujjhanti| sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai adhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai dhammai byāpaj‍jati, anupaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati parittacaitasau| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, bhikkhu avassutau cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu…pai॰… avassutau manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃvihāriṃ cāvusau, bhikkhuṃ cakkhutau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, labhaithaiva labhataiva (syā॰ ka॰) aivamuparipi mārau autāraṃ  labhaitha labhati (syā॰ ka॰) aivamuparipi mārau ārammaṇaṃ, sautatau caipi naṃ…pai॰… manatau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, labhaithaiva mārau autāraṃ labhaitha mārau ārammaṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, naḷāgāraṃ vā tiṇāgāraṃ vā sukkhaṃ kauḷāpaṃ kaulāpaṃ (syā॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.243 tairauvassikaṃ| puratthimāya caipi naṃ disāya purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya upasaṅkamaiyya, labhaithaiva aggi autāraṃ labhaitha aggi ārammaṇaṃ; pacchimāya caipi naṃ disāya…pai॰… uttarāya caipi naṃ disāya… dakkhiṇāya caipi naṃ disāya… haiṭṭhimatau pacchatau (syā॰), haiṭṭhimāya (ka॰) caipi naṃ disāya… uparimatau uparitau (syā॰), uparimāya (ka॰) caipi naṃ disāya… yatau kutauci caipi naṃ purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya upasaṅkamaiyya, labhaithaiva aggi autāraṃ labhaitha aggi ārammaṇaṃ| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, aivaṃvihāriṃ bhikkhuṃ cakkhutau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, labhaithaiva mārau autāraṃ labhaitha mārau ārammaṇaṃ, sautatau caipi  naṃ…pai॰… manatau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, labhaithaiva mārau autāraṃ labhaitha mārau ārammaṇaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃvihāriṃ cāvusau, bhikkhuṃ rūpā adhibhaṃsu abhibhaviṃsu (syā॰), abhibhaṃsu (ka॰) aivamuparipi, na bhikkhu rūpai adhibhausi; saddā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu saddai adhibhausi; gandhā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu gandhai adhibhausi; rasā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu rasai adhibhausi; phauṭṭhabbā bhikkhuṃ  adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu phauṭṭhabbai adhibhausi; dhammā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu, na bhikkhu dhammai adhibhausi| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, bhikkhu rūpādhibhūtau saddādhibhūtau gandhādhibhūtau rasādhibhūtau phauṭṭhabbādhibhūtau dhammādhibhūtau adhibhū anadhibhūtau adhibhaṃsu naṃ pāpakā akusalā dhammā saṃkilaisikā paunaubhavikā sadarā dukkhavipākā āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyā| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, avassutau hauti|

‘‘kathaṃ cāvusau, anavassutau hauti? idhāvusau, bhikkhu cakkhunā rūpaṃ disvā piyarūpai rūpai nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai rūpai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati appamāṇacaitasau| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti; sautaina saddaṃ sutvā…pai॰… manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya piyarūpai dhammai nādhimuc‍cati, appiyarūpai dhammai na byāpaj‍jati, upaṭṭhitakāyassati ca viharati appamāṇacaitasau| tañ‍ca caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yatthassa tai uppan‍nā pāpakā akusalā dhammā aparisaisā nirujjhanti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, bhikkhu anavassutau cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaisu rūpaisu…pai॰… anavassutau manauviñ‍ñaiyyaisu dhammaisu| aivaṃvihāriṃ cāvusau, bhikkhuṃ cakkhutau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, naiva labhaitha mārau autāraṃ, na labhaitha mārau ārammaṇaṃ; sautatau caipi naṃ…pai॰… manatau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, naiva labhaitha  mārau autāraṃ, na labhaitha mārau ārammaṇaṃ|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, kūṭāgārā vā kūṭāgārasālā santhāgārasālā (syā॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.243 vā bahalamattikā addāvalaipanā al‍lāvalaipanā (syā॰) puratthimāya caipi naṃ disāya purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya upasaṅkamaiyya, naiva labhaitha aggi autāraṃ, na labhaitha aggi ārammaṇaṃ; pacchimāya caipi naṃ disāya… uttarāya caipi naṃ disāya… dakkhiṇāya caipi naṃ disāya… haiṭṭhimatau caipi naṃ disāya… uparimatau caipi naṃ disāya… yatau kutauci caipi naṃ purisau ādittāya tiṇuk‍kāya upasaṅkamaiyya, naiva labhaitha aggi autāraṃ na labhaitha aggi ārammaṇaṃ| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, aivaṃvihāriṃ bhikkhuṃ  cakkhutau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati, naiva labhaitha mārau autāraṃ na labhaitha mārau ārammaṇaṃ; sautatau caipi naṃ…pai॰… manatau caipi naṃ mārau upasaṅkamati naiva labhaitha mārau autāraṃ na labhaitha mārau ārammaṇaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃvihārī cāvusau, bhikkhu rūpai adhibhausi abhibhaviṃsu (syā॰), abhibhausi (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.243, na rūpā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; saddai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na saddā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; gandhai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na gandhā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; rasai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na rasā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; phauṭṭhabbai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na phauṭṭhabbā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu; dhammai bhikkhu adhibhausi, na dhammā bhikkhuṃ adhibhaṃsu| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, bhikkhu rūpādhibhū saddādhibhū gandhādhibhū rasādhibhū phauṭṭhabbādhibhū dhammādhibhū adhibhū anadhibhūtau anabhibhūtau kaihi ci kilaisaihi (ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.243| adhibhausi tai pāpakai akusalai dhammai saṃkilaisikai paunaubhavikai sadarai dukkhavipākai āyatiṃ jātijarāmaraṇiyai| aivaṃ khau, āvusau, anavassutau hautī’’ti – anavassutau|

apariḍayhamānauti rāgajaina pariḷāhaina apariḍayhamānau, dausajaina pariḷāhaina apariḍayhamānau, mauhajaina pariḷāhaina apariḍayhamānauti  – anavassutau apariḍayhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘aukkhittacakkhu na ca pādalaulau, guttindriyau rakkhitamānasānau|

anavassutau apariḍayhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

150.

 auhārayitvā gihibyañ‍janāni, sañchan‍napattau yathā pārichattakau|

kāsāyavatthau abhinikkhamitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

auhārayitvā gihibyañ‍janānīti gihibyañ‍janāni vuc‍canti kaisā ca massū ca…pai॰… dīghadasāni iti vā| auhārayitvā gihibyañ‍janānīti gihibyañ‍janāni auraupayitvā samauraupayitvā nikkhipitvā paṭippassambhitvāti – auhārayitvā gihibyañ‍janāni|

sañchan‍napattau yathā pārichattakauti yathā sau pārichattakau kauviḷārau bahalapattapalāsau sākhapattapalāsau (ka॰) sandacchāyau saṇḍacchāyau (syā॰), santacchāyau (ka॰) passa ma॰ ni॰ 1.154, aivamaiva sau pac‍caikasambuddhau paripuṇṇapattacīvaradharauti – sañchan‍napattau yathā pārichattakau|

kāsāyavatthau  abhinikkhamitvāti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau sabbaṃ gharāvāsapalibaudhaṃ chinditvā puttadāraṃ palibaudhaṃ chinditvā ñātipalibaudhaṃ  chinditvā mittāmac‍capalibaudhaṃ chinditvā san‍nidhipalibaudhaṃ chinditvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā auhārayitvā (syā॰ ka॰) kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitvā akiñ‍canabhāvaṃ upagantvā aikau carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaitīti – kāsāyavatthau abhinikkhamitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘auhārayitvā gihibyañ‍janāni, sañchan‍napattau yathā pārichattakau|

kāsāyavatthau abhinikkhamitvā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

tatiyau vaggau|

catutthavaggau

151.

 rasaisu gaidhaṃ akaraṃ alaulau, anañ‍ñapausī sapadānacārī|

kulai kulai appaṭibaddhacittau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

rasaisu gaidhaṃ akaraṃ alaulauti| rasauti mūlarasau khandharasau tacarasau pattarasau puppharasau phalarasau, ambilaṃ madhuraṃ tittakaṃ kaṭukaṃ lauṇikaṃ khārikaṃ lambikaṃ lambilaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) passa āyatanavibhaṅgai kasāvau sādu asādu sītaṃ uṇhaṃ| santaikai samaṇabrāhmaṇā rasagiddhā| tai jivhaggaina rasaggāni pariyaisantā āhiṇḍanti| tai ambilaṃ labhitvā anambilaṃ pariyaisanti, anambilaṃ labhitvā ambilaṃ pariyaisanti; madhuraṃ labhitvā amadhuraṃ pariyaisanti, amadhuraṃ labhitvā madhuraṃ pariyaisanti; tittakaṃ labhitvā atittakaṃ  pariyaisanti, atittakaṃ labhitvā tittakaṃ pariyaisanti; kaṭukaṃ labhitvā akuṭakaṃ pariyaisanti, akuṭakaṃ labhitvā kaṭukaṃ pariyaisanti; lauṇikaṃ labhitvā alauṇikaṃ pariyaisanti, alauṇikaṃ labhitvā lauṇikaṃ pariyaisanti; khārikaṃ labhitvā akhārikaṃ pariyaisanti, akhārikaṃ labhitvā khārikaṃ pariyaisanti; kasāvaṃ labhitvā akasāvaṃ pariyaisanti, akasāvaṃ labhitvā kasāvaṃ pariyaisanti; lambikaṃ labhitvā alambikaṃ pariyaisanti, alambikaṃ labhitvā lambikaṃ pariyaisanti; sāduṃ labhitvā asāduṃ pariyaisanti, asāduṃ labhitvā  sāduṃ pariyaisanti; sītaṃ labhitvā uṇhaṃ pariyaisanti, uṇhaṃ labhitvā sītaṃ pariyaisanti| tai yaṃ yaṃ labhanti taina taina na tussanti, aparāparaṃ pariyaisanti| manāpikaisu rasaisu rattā giddhā gathitā mucchitā ajjhausan‍nā laggā laggitā palibuddhā| sā rasataṇhā tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā sau pac‍caikasambuddhau paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘‘naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya; yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya| iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’’ti|

yathā vaṇaṃ ālimpaiyya yāvadaiva āruhaṇatthāya raupanatthāya (syā॰), yathā vā akkhaṃ abbhañ‍jaiyya yāvadaiva bhārassa nittharaṇatthāya, yathā puttamaṃsaṃ āhāraṃ āharaiyya yāvadaiva kantārassa nittharaṇatthāya; aivamaiva sau pac‍caikasambuddhau  paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau āhāraṃ āhāraiti – ‘‘naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya; yāvadaiva imassa kāyassa ṭhitiyā yāpanāya vihiṃsūparatiyā brahmacariyānuggahāya| iti purāṇañ‍ca vaidanaṃ paṭihaṅkhāmi, navañ‍ca vaidanaṃ na uppādaissāmi, yātrā ca mai bhavissati anavaj‍jatā ca phāsuvihārau cā’’ti| rasataṇhāya āratau viratau paṭiviratau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharatīti – rasaisu gaidhaṃ akaraṃ|

alaulauti lauluppaṃ vuc‍cati taṇhā| yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| sā lauluppā taṇhā tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā pac‍caikasambuddhau alaulauti – rasaisu gaidhaṃ akaraṃ alaulau|

anañ‍ñapausī sapadānacārīti anañ‍ñapausīti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau attānañ‍ñaiva pausaiti, na paranti|

anañ‍ñapausimañ‍ñātaṃ, dantaṃ sārai patiṭṭhitaṃ sāraisu supatiṭṭhitaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 6|

khīṇāsavaṃ vantadausaṃ, tamahaṃ brūmi brāhmaṇanti||

anañ‍ñapausī  sapadānacārīti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ pubbanhasamayaṃ (ka॰) nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisati rakkhitainaiva kāyaina rakkhitāya vācāya rakkhitaina cittaina upaṭṭhitāya satiyā saṃvutaihi indriyaihi| aukkhittacakkhu iriyāpathasampan‍nau kulā  kulaṃ anatik‍kamantau piṇḍāya caratīti – anañ‍ñapausī sapadānacārī|

kulai kulai appaṭibaddhacittauti dvīhi kāraṇaihi paṭibaddhacittau hauti – attānaṃ vā nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti, attānaṃ vā uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti| kathaṃ attānaṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti? ‘‘tumhai mai bahūpakārā, ahaṃ tumhai nissāya labhāmi cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ| yampi mai añ‍ñai dātuṃ vā kātuṃ vā mañ‍ñanti tumhai nissāya tumhai passantā| yampi mai paurāṇaṃ mātāpaittikaṃ nāmagauttaṃ tampi mai antarahitaṃ tumhaihi ahaṃ ñāyāmi – ‘asukassa kulupakau, asukāya kulupakau’’’ti| aivaṃ attānaṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti|

kathaṃ attānaṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti? ‘‘ahaṃ tumhākaṃ bahūpakārau, tumhai maṃ āgamma buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gatā, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratā, kāmaisumicchācārā paṭiviratā, musāvādā paṭiviratā, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratā, tumhākaṃ ahaṃ uddaisaṃ daimi paripucchaṃ daimi upausathaṃ ācikkhāmi navakammaṃ adhiṭṭhāmi; atha ca pana tumhai maṃ ujjhitvā añ‍ñai sak‍karautha garuṃ karautha mānaitha  pūjaithā’’ti| aivaṃ attānaṃ uc‍caṃ ṭhapaintau paraṃ nīcaṃ ṭhapaintau paṭibaddhacittau hauti|

kulai kulai appaṭibaddhacittauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau kulapalibaudhaina appaṭibaddhacittau hauti, gaṇapalibaudhaina appaṭibaddhacittau hauti, āvāsapalibaudhaina appaṭibaddhacittau hauti, cīvarapalibaudhaina appaṭibaddhacittau hauti, piṇḍapātapalibaudhaina appaṭibaddhacittau hauti, saināsanapalibaudhaina  appaṭibaddhacittau hauti, gilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārapalibaudhaina appaṭibaddhacittau hautīti – kulai kulai appaṭibaddhacittau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘rasaisu  gaidhaṃ akaraṃ alaulau, anañ‍ñapausī sapadānacārī|

kulai kulai appaṭibaddhacittau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

152.

 pahāya pañ‍cāvaraṇāni caitasau, upak‍kilaisai byapanuj‍ja sabbai|

anissitau chaitva chaitvā (syā॰ ka॰) sinaihadausaṃ snaihadausaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

pahāya pañ‍cāvaraṇāni caitasauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ pahāya pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ  gamaitvā, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ… thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ… uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ… vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ pahāya pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatīti – pahāya pañ‍cāvaraṇāni caitasau|

upak‍kilaisai byapanuj‍ja sabbaiti rāgau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, dausau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, mauhau cittassa upak‍kilaisau, kaudhau… upanāhau…pai॰… sabbākusalābhisaṅkhārā cittassa upak‍kilaisā| upak‍kilaisai byapanuj‍ja sabbaiti sabbai cittassa upak‍kilaisai byapanuj‍ja panuditvā pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvāti – upak‍kilaisai byapanuj‍ja sabbai|

anissitau  chaitva sinaihadausanti| anissitauti dvai nissayā – taṇhānissayau ca diṭṭhinissayau ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhānissayau…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhinissayau| sinaihauti dvai snaihā – taṇhāsnaihau ca diṭṭhisnaihau ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhāsnaihau…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhisnaihau| dausauti yau cittassa āghātau paṭighātau paṭighaṃ paṭiviraudhau kaupau pakaupau sampakaupau dausau padausau sampadausau cittassa byāpatti manaupadausau kaudhau kujjhanā kujjhitattaṃ dausau dussanā dussitattaṃ byāpatti byāpaj‍janā byāpaj‍jitattaṃ caṇḍik‍kaṃ asuraupau anattamanatā cittassa| anissitau chaitva sinaihadausanti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau taṇhāsnaihañ‍ca diṭṭhisnaihañ‍ca dausañ‍ca chaitvā ucchinditvā samucchinditvā pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvā cakkhuṃ anissitau, sautaṃ anissitau…pai॰… diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbai dhammai anissitau anal‍līnau anupagatau anajjhausitau anadhimuttau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharatīti – anissitau chaitva sinaihadausaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘pahāya  pañ‍cāvaraṇāni caitasau, upak‍kilaisai byapanuj‍ja sabbai|

anissitau chaitva sinaihadausaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

153.

 vipiṭṭhikatvāna sukhaṃ dukhañ‍ca, pubbaiva ca saumanassadaumanassaṃ|

laddhānupaikkhaṃ samathaṃ visuddhaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

vipiṭṭhikatvāna sukhaṃ dukhañ‍ca, pubbaiva ca saumanassadaumanassanti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatīti – vipiṭṭhikatvāna  sukhaṃ dukhañ‍ca, pubbaiva ca saumanassadaumanassaṃ|

laddhānupaikkhaṃ  samathaṃ visuddhanti| upaikkhāti yā catutthajjhānai upaikkhā upaikkhanā ajjhupaikkhanā cittasamatā cittappassaddhatā cittavisaṭatā (ka॰) passa mahāni॰ 207 majjhattatā cittassa| samathauti yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti avisāhārau avisaṃhārau (ka॰) passa dha॰ sa॰ 11, 15 avikkhaipau avisāhaṭamānasatā avisaṃhaṭamānasatā (ka॰) samathau samādhindriyaṃ samādhibalaṃ sammāsamādhi; catutthajjhānai upaikkhā ca samathau ca suddhā haunti visuddhā pariyaudātā anaṅgaṇā vigatūpak‍kilaisā mudubhūtā kammaniyā ṭhitā ānaiñ‍jappattā| laddhānupaikkhaṃ samathaṃ visuddhanti catutthajjhānaṃ upaikkhañ‍ca samathañ‍ca laddhā labhitvā vinditvā paṭilabhitvāti – laddhānupaikkhaṃ samathaṃ visuddhaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘vipiṭṭhikatvāna sukhaṃ dukhañ‍ca, pubbaiva ca saumanassadaumanassaṃ|

laddhānupaikkhaṃ samathaṃ visuddhaṃ, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

154.

 āraddhavīriyau paramatthapattiyā, alīnacittau akusītavutti|

daḷhanik‍kamau thāmabalūpapan‍nau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

āraddhavīriyau paramatthapattiyāti paramatthaṃ vuc‍cati amataṃ  nibbānaṃ| yau sau sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānaṃ| paramatthassa pattiyā lābhāya paṭilābhāya adhigamāya phassanāya sacchikiriyāya āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ  dhammānaṃ pahānāya kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ sampadāya thāmavā thāmasā (ka॰) daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisūti – āraddhavīriyau paramatthapattiyā|

alīnacittau akusītavuttīti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya…pai॰… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ  dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā bhāvanāpāripūriyā (syā॰) chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahatīti – aivaṃ alīnacittau akusītavutti|

atha vā, ‘‘kāmaṃ tacau ca nhāru ca aṭṭhi ca avasissatu avasussatu (syā॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.184; saṃ॰ ni॰ 2.22 passitabbaṃ, sarīrai upasussatu maṃsalauhitaṃ, yaṃ taṃ purisathāmaina purisabalaina purisavīriyaina purisaparak‍kamaina pattabbaṃ na taṃ apāpuṇitvā vīriyassa saṇṭhānaṃ viriyassa ṭhānaṃ (syā॰) bhavissatī’’ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivampi alīnacittau akusītavutti|

nāsissaṃ na pivissāmi, vihāratau na nikkhamai|

napi passaṃ nipātaissaṃ, taṇhāsal‍lai anūhataiti||

cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivampi alīnacittau akusītavutti|

‘‘na tāvāhaṃ imaṃ pal‍laṅkaṃ bhindissāmi yāva mai na  anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cissatī’’ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivampi alīnacittau akusītavutti|

‘‘na tāvāhaṃ imamhā āsanā vuṭṭhahissāmi yāva mai na anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cissatī’’ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivampi alīnacittau akusītavutti|

‘‘na tāvāhaṃ imamhā caṅkamā aurauhissāmi…pai॰… vihārā nikkhamissāmi… aḍḍhayaugā nikkhamissāmi… pāsādā nikkhamissāmi… hammiyā nikkhamissāmi… guhāya nikkhamissāmi… laiṇā nikkhamissāmi… kuṭiyā nikkhamissāmi… kūṭāgārā nikkhamissāmi… aṭṭā nikkhamissāmi… māḷā nikkhamissāmi… uddaṇḍā nikkhamissāmi … upaṭṭhānasālāya nikkhamissāmi… maṇḍapā nikkhamissāmi… rukkhamūlā nikkhamissāmi yāva mai na anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cissatī’’ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivampi alīnacittau akusītavutti|

‘‘imasmiṃyaiva pubbaṇhasamayai pubbaṇhasamayaṃ (ka॰) mahāni॰ 17 ariyadhammaṃ āharissāmi samāharissāmi adhigacchissāmi phassayissāmi sacchikarissāmī’’ti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivampi alīnacittau akusītavutti| imasmiṃyaiva majjhanhikasamayai…pai॰… sāyanhasamayai …pai॰… puraibhattaṃ… pacchābhattaṃ… purimayāmaṃ… majjhimayāmaṃ… pacchimayāmaṃ… kāḷai… juṇhai… vassai… haimantai … gimhai… purimai vayaukhandhai… majjhimai vayaukhandhai… pacchimai vayaukhandhai ariyadhammaṃ āharissāmi samāharissāmi adhigacchissāmi phassayissāmi sacchikarissāmīti cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivampi alīnacittau akusītavutti|

daḷhanik‍kamau thāmabalūpapan‍nauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau daḷhasamādānau ahausi kusalaisu dhammaisu avaṭṭhitasamādānau kāyasucaritai vacīsucaritai manausucaritai dānasaṃvibhāgai sīlasamādānai upausathupavāsai mattaiyyatāya maittaiyyatāya (syā॰ ka॰) mauggal‍lānabyākaraṇai 4.39 suttaṃ aulaukaitabbaṃ paittaiyyatāya sāmañ‍ñatāya brahmañ‍ñatāya kulaijaiṭṭhāpacāyitāya añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraisu adhikusalaisu dhammaisūti – daḷhanik‍kamau| thāmabalūpapan‍nauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau thāmaina ca balaina ca vīriyaina ca parak‍kamaina ca pañ‍ñāya ca upaitau hauti samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau samupapan‍nau saman‍nāgatauti – daḷhanik‍kamau thāmabalūpapan‍nau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘āraddhavīriyau paramatthapattiyā, alīnacittau akusītavutti|

daḷhanik‍kamau thāmabalūpapan‍nau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

155.

 paṭisal‍lānaṃ jhānamariñ‍camānau, dhammaisu nic‍caṃ anudhammacārī|

ādīnavaṃ  sammasitā bhavaisu, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

paṭisal‍lānaṃ jhānamariñ‍camānauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau paṭisal‍lānārāmau hauti paṭisal‍lānaratau ajjhattaṃ caitausamathamanuyuttau anirākatajjhānau vipassanāya saman‍nāgatau brūhaitā suñ‍ñāgāraṃ jhāyī jhānaratau aikattamanuyuttau sadatthagarukauti paṭisal‍lānaṃ| jhānamariñ‍camānauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau dvīhi kāraṇaihi jhānaṃ na riñ‍cati – anuppan‍nassa vā paṭhamassa jhānassa uppādāya  yuttau payuttau saṃyuttau  āyuttau samāyuttau, anuppan‍nassa vā dutiyassa jhānassa…pai॰… anuppan‍nassa vā tatiyassa jhānassa… anuppan‍nassa vā catutthassa jhānassa uppādāya yuttau payuttau saṃyuttau āyuttau samāyuttauti – aivampi jhānaṃ na riñ‍cati|

atha vā, uppan‍naṃ vā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti, uppan‍naṃ vā dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ…pai॰… uppan‍naṃ vā tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… uppan‍naṃ vā catutthaṃ jhānaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti| aivampi jhānaṃ na riñ‍catīti – paṭisal‍lānaṃ jhānamariñ‍camānau|

dhammaisu nic‍caṃ anudhammacārīti dhammā vuc‍canti cattārau satipaṭṭhānā…pai॰… ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| katamai anudhammā? sammāpaṭipadā apac‍canīkapaṭipadā anvatthapaṭipadā dhammānudhammapaṭipadā sīlaisu paripūrakāritā indriyaisu guttadvāratā bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā jāgariyānuyaugau satisampajañ‍ñaṃ – imai vuc‍canti anudhammā| dhammaisu nic‍caṃ anudhammacārīti dhammaisu nic‍cakālaṃ dhuvakālaṃ satataṃ samitaṃ avaukiṇṇaṃ pauṅkhānupauṅkhaṃ udakūmikajātaṃ udakummijātaṃ (syā॰), udakummikajātaṃ (ka॰)

avīcisantatisahitaṃ  phassitaṃ puraibhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ purimayāmaṃ majjhimayāmaṃ pacchimayāmaṃ kāḷai juṇhai vassai haimantai gimhai purimai vayaukhandhai majjhimai vayaukhandhai pacchimai vayaukhandhai carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaitīti – dhammaisu nic‍caṃ anudhammacārī|

ādīnavaṃ sammasitā bhavaisūti ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti ādīnavaṃ sammasitā bhavaisu, ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti…pai॰… ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti ādīnavaṃ sammasitā bhavaisūti – ādīnavaṃ sammasitā bhavaisu, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘paṭisal‍lānaṃ jhānamariñ‍camānau, dhammaisu nic‍caṃ anudhammacārī|

ādīnavaṃ sammasitā bhavaisu, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

156.

 taṇhakkhayaṃ  patthayamappamattau, anaiḷamūgau anailamūgau (syā॰ ka॰) sutavā satīmā|

saṅkhātadhammau niyatau padhānavā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

taṇhakkhayaṃ patthayamappamattauti| taṇhāti rūpataṇhā…pai॰… dhammataṇhā| taṇhakkhayanti  rāgakkhayaṃ dausakkhayaṃ mauhakkhayaṃ gatikkhayaṃ upapattikkhayaṃ paṭisandhikkhayaṃ bhavakkhayaṃ saṃsārakkhayaṃ  vaṭṭakkhayaṃ patthayantau icchantau sādiyantau pihayantau abhijappantauti – taṇhakkhayaṃ patthayaṃ| appamattauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau sak‍kac‍cakārī sātac‍cakārī…pai॰… appamādau kusalaisu dhammaisūti – taṇhakkhayaṃ patthayamappamattau|

anaiḷamūgau sutavā satīmāti| anaiḷamūgauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau paṇḍitau pañ‍ñavā buddhimā ñāṇī vibhāvī maidhāvī| sutavāti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nic‍cayau| yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpāssa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā| satīmāti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau satimā hauti paramaina satinaipak‍kaina saman‍nāgatattā cirakatampi cirabhāsitampi saritā anussaritāti – anaiḷamūgau sutavā satīmā|

saṅkhātadhammau niyatau padhānavāti saṅkhātadhammau vuc‍cati ñāṇaṃ| yā pañ‍ñā pajānanā…pai॰… amauhau dhammavicayau sammādiṭṭhi| saṅkhātadhammauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau saṅkhātadhammau ñātadhammau tulitadhammau tīritadhammau vibhūtadhammau vibhāvitadhammau ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā’’ti saṅkhātadhammau…pai॰… ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ sabbaṃ  taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti saṅkhātadhammau ñātadhammau tulitadhammau tīritadhammau vibhūtadhammau vibhāvitadhammau| atha vā, tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa ca khandhā saṃkhittā dhātuyau saṃkhittā āyatanāni saṃkhittāni gatiyau saṃkhittā upapattiyau saṃkhittā paṭisandhiyau saṃkhittā bhavā saṃkhittā saṃsārā saṃkhittā vaṭṭā saṃkhittā| atha vā, sau pac‍caikasambuddhau khandhapariyantai ṭhitau dhātupariyantai  ṭhitau āyatanapariyantai ṭhitau gatipariyantai ṭhitau upapattipariyantai ṭhitau paṭisandhipariyantai ṭhitau bhavapariyantai ṭhitau saṃsārapariyantai ṭhitau vaṭṭapariyantai ṭhitau saṅkhārapariyantai ṭhitau antimabhavai antimai bhavai (ka॰) cūḷani॰ maittagūmāṇavapucchāniddaisa 28 ṭhitau antimasamussayai ṭhitau antimadaihadharau pac‍caikasambuddhau|

tassāyaṃ pacchimakau bhavau, carimauyaṃ samussayau|

jātimaraṇasaṃsārau jātijarāmaraṇasaṃsārau (syā॰) aivamīdisaisu ṭhānaisu, natthi tassa punabbhavauti||

taṃkāraṇā pac‍caikasambuddhau saṅkhātadhammau| niyatauti niyāmā vuc‍canti cattārau ariyamaggā| catūhi ariyamaggaihi saman‍nāgatauti niyatau| niyāmaṃ pattau sampattau adhigatau phassitau sacchikatau pattau niyāmaṃ| padhānavāti padhānaṃ vuc‍cati vīriyaṃ| sau caitasau vīriyārambhau  nik‍kamau parak‍kamau uyyāmau vāyāmau ussāhau ussauḷhī thāmau dhiti asithilaparak‍kamau anikkhittacchandatā anikkhittadhuratā dhurasampaggāhau vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ vīriyabalaṃ sammāvāyāmau| sau pac‍caikasambuddhau iminā padhānaina upaitau samupaitau upāgatau samupāgatau upapan‍nau samupapan‍nau saman‍nāgatau| tasmā  sau pac‍caikasambuddhau padhānavāti – saṅkhātadhammau niyatau padhānavā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘taṇhakkhayaṃ patthayamappamattau, anaiḷamūgau sutavā satīmā|

saṅkhātadhammau niyatau padhānavā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

157.

 sīhauva saddaisu asantasantau, vātauva jālamhi asaj‍jamānau|

padumaṃva tauyaina alimpamānau alippamānau, su॰ ni॰ 71, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

sīhauva saddaisu asantasantauti yathā sīhau migarājā saddaisu asantāsī aparisantāsī anutrāsī anubbiggau anussaṅkī anussukī (syā॰) anutrāsau  abhīrū acchambhī anutrāsī apalāyī, pac‍caikasambuddhaupi saddaisu asantāsī aparisantāsī anutrāsī anubbiggau anussaṅkī anutrāsau abhīrū acchambhī anutrāsī apalāyī pahīnabhayabhairavau vigatalaumahaṃsau viharatīti – sīhauva saddaisu asantasantau|

vātauva jālamhi asaj‍jamānauti| vātauti puratthimā vātā pacchimā vātā uttarā vātā dakkhiṇā vātā sarajā vātā arajā vātā sītā vātā uṇhā vātā parittā vātā adhimattā vātā vairambhavātā  pakkhavātā pakkhivātā (syā॰) cūḷani॰ upasīvamāṇavapucchāniddaisa 43, khaggavisāṇasuttaniddaisa 138 natthi pāṭhanānattaṃ supaṇṇavātā tālapaṇṇavātā vidhūpanavātā| jālaṃ vuc‍cati suttajālaṃ| yathā vātau jālamhi na saj‍jati na gaṇhāti na bajjhati na palibajjhati, aivamaiva dvai jālā – taṇhājālañ‍ca diṭṭhijālañ‍ca…pai॰… idaṃ taṇhājālaṃ…pai॰… idaṃ diṭṭhijālaṃ| tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa taṇhājālaṃ pahīnaṃ diṭṭhijālaṃ paṭinissaṭṭhaṃ, taṇhājālassa pahīnattā diṭṭhijālassa paṭinissaṭṭhattā sau pac‍caikasambuddhau rūpai na saj‍jati saddai na saj‍jati…pai॰… diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu na saj‍jati na gaṇhāti na bajjhati na palibajjhati nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina caitasā viharatīti – vātauva jālamhi asaj‍jamānau|

padumaṃva  tauyaina alimpamānauti padumaṃ vuc‍cati padumapupphaṃ| tauyaṃ vuc‍cati udakaṃ| yathā padumapupphaṃ tauyaina na limpati na palimpati na upalimpati, alittaṃ apalittaṃ anupalittaṃ, aivamaiva dvai laipā – taṇhālaipau ca diṭṭhilaipau ca…pai॰… ayaṃ taṇhālaipau…pai॰… ayaṃ diṭṭhilaipau| tassa pac‍caikasambuddhassa taṇhālaipau pahīnau, diṭṭhilaipau paṭinissaṭṭhau| taṇhālaipassa pahīnattā diṭṭhilaipassa paṭinisaṭṭhattā sau pac‍caikasambuddhau rūpai na limpati saddai na limpati…pai॰… diṭṭhasutamutaviñ‍ñātabbaisu dhammaisu na limpati na palimpati na upalimpati, alittau apalittau anupalittau nikkhantau nissaṭau vippamuttau visañ‍ñuttau vimariyādikataina  caitasā viharatīti – padumaṃva tauyaina alimpamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘sīhauva  saddaisu asantasantau, vātauva jālamhi asaj‍jamānau|

padumaṃva tauyaina alimpamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

158.

 sīhau yathā dāṭhabalī pasayha, rājā migānaṃ abhibhuyya cārī|

saivaitha pantāni saināsanāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

sīhau yathā dāṭhabalī pasayha, rājā migānaṃ abhibhuyya cārīti yathā sīhau migarājā dāṭhabalī dāṭhāvudhau sabbai tiracchānagatai pāṇai abhibhuyya abhibhavitvā ajjhauttharitvā pariyādiyitvā madditvā carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaiti, pac‍caikasambuddhaupi pañ‍ñābalī pañ‍ñāvudhau sabbapāṇabhūtai puggalai pañ‍ñāya abhibhuyya abhibhavitvā ajjhauttharitvā pariyādiyitvā madditvā carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaitīti – sīhau yathā dāṭhabalī pasayha, rājā migānaṃ abhibhuyya cārī|

saivaitha pantāni saināsanānīti yathā sīhau migarājā arañ‍ñavanamajjhaugāhaitvā carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti  yapaiti yāpaiti, pac‍caikasambuddhaupi arañ‍ñavanapatthāni pantāni saināsanāni paṭisaivati appasaddāni appanigghausāni vijanavātāni manussarāhassaiyyakāni paṭisal‍lānasāruppāni| sau aikau gacchati aikau tiṭṭhati aikau nisīdati aikau saiyyaṃ kappaiti aikau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisati aikau paṭik‍kamati aikau rahau nisīdati aikau caṅkamaṃ adhiṭṭhāti aikau carati viharati iriyati vattaiti pālaiti yapaiti yāpaitīti – saivaitha pantāni saināsanāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘sīhau  yathā dāṭhabalī pasayha, rājā migānaṃ abhibhuyya cārī|

saivaitha pantāni saināsanāni, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

159.

 maittaṃ  upaikkhaṃ karuṇaṃ vimuttiṃ, āsaivamānau muditañ‍ca kālai|

sabbaina laukaina avirujjhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

maittaṃ upaikkhaṃ karuṇaṃ vimuttiṃ, āsaivamānau muditañ‍ca kālaiti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ, iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina  caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharati; karuṇāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… muditāsahagataina caitasā…pai॰… upaikkhāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina pharitvā viharatīti – maittaṃ upaikkhaṃ karuṇaṃ vimuttiṃ, āsaivamānau muditañ‍ca kālai|

sabbaina laukaina avirujjhamānauti maittāya bhāvitattā yai puratthimāya disāya sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti, yai pacchimāya disāya sattā…pai॰… yai uttarāya disāya sattā… yai dakkhiṇāya disāya sattā… yai puratthimāya anudisāya sattā… yai pacchimāya anudisāya sattā… yai uttarāya anudisāya sattā… yai dakkhiṇāya anudisāya sattā… yai haiṭṭhimāya adhaugamāya (syā॰) disāya sattā… yai uparimāya disāya sattā… yai dasasu disāsu sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti| karuṇāya bhāvitattā… muditāya bhāvitattā… upaikkhāya bhāvitattā yai puratthimāya disāya sattā…pai॰… yai dasasu disāsu sattā tai appaṭikūlā haunti| sabbaina laukaina avirujjhamānauti sabbaina laukaina avirujjhamānau, appaṭivirujjhamānau anāghātiyamānau aghaṭṭiyamānau (syā॰) appaṭihañ‍ñamānauti – sabbaina laukaina avirujjhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘maittaṃ upaikkhaṃ karuṇaṃ vimuttiṃ, āsaivamānau  muditañ‍ca kālai|

sabbaina laukaina avirujjhamānau, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

160.

 rāgañ‍ca  dausañ‍ca pahāya mauhaṃ, sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanāni saṃyaujanāni (ka॰) |

asantasaṃ jīvitasaṅkhayamhi, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca pahāya mauhanti| rāgauti yau rāgau sārāgau…pai॰… abhijjhā laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| dausauti yau cittassa āghātau…pai॰… caṇḍik‍kaṃ asuraupau anattamanatā cittassa| mauhauti dukkhai añ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jālaṅgī mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca pahāya mauhanti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca mauhañ‍ca pahāya pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvāti – rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca pahāya mauhaṃ|

sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanānīti| dasa saṃyaujanāni – kāmarāgasaṃyaujanaṃ paṭighasaṃyaujanaṃ…pai॰… avij‍jāsaṃyaujanaṃ| sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanānīti dasa saṃyaujanāni sandālayitvā padālayitvā sampadālayitvā pajahitvā vinaudaitvā byantīkaritvā anabhāvaṃ gamaitvāti – sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanāni|

asantasaṃ  jīvitasaṅkhayamhīti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau jīvitapariyausānai asantāsī anutrāsī anubbiggau anussaṅkī anutrāsau abhīrū acchambhī anutrāsī apalāyī pahīnabhayabhairavau vigatalaumahaṃsau viharatīti – asantasaṃ jīvitasaṅkhayamhi, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘rāgañ‍ca dausañ‍ca pahāya mauhaṃ, sandālayitvāna saṃyaujanāni|

asantasaṃ jīvitasaṅkhayamhi, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

161.

 bhajanti saivanti ca kāraṇatthā, nik‍kāraṇā dul‍labhā aj‍ja mittā|

attatthapañ‍ñā attaṭṭhapañ‍ñā (ka॰) asucī manussā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau||

bhajanti  saivanti ca kāraṇatthāti attatthakāraṇā paratthakāraṇā ubhayatthakāraṇā diṭṭhadhammikatthakāraṇā samparāyikatthakāraṇā paramatthakāraṇā bhajanti sambhajanti saivanti nisaivanti saṃsaivanti paṭisaivantīti – bhajanti saivanti ca kāraṇatthā|

nik‍kāraṇā  dul‍labhā aj‍ja mittāti dvai mittā – agārikamittau ca anāgārikamittau ca…pai॰… ayaṃ agārikamittau…pai॰… ayaṃ anāgārikamittau| nik‍kāraṇā dul‍labhā aj‍ja mittāti imai  dvai mittā akāraṇā nik‍kāraṇā ahaitū appac‍cayā dul‍labhā (dul‍laddhā sudul‍laddhā) ( ) natthi syā॰ pautthakai ti – nik‍kāraṇā dul‍labhā aj‍ja mittā|

attatthapañ‍ñā asucī manussāti| attatthapañ‍ñāti attanau atthāya attanau haitu attanau pac‍cayā attanau kāraṇā bhajanti sambhajanti saivanti nisaivanti saṃsaivanti paṭisaivanti ācaranti samācaranti payirupāsanti paripucchanti paripañhantīti – attatthapañ‍ñā| asucī manussāti asucinā kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatāti asucī manussā, asucinā vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatāti asucī manussā, asucinā manaukammaina saman‍nāgatāti asucī manussā, asucinā pāṇātipātaina…pai॰… asucinā adin‍nādānaina… asucinā kāmaisumicchācāraina… asucinā musāvādaina… asuciyā pisuṇāya vācāya saman‍nāgatā… asuciyā pharusāya vācāya saman‍nāgatā… asucinā samphappalāpaina saman‍nāgatā… asuciyā abhijjhāya saman‍nāgatā… asucinā byāpādaina saman‍nāgatāti asucī manussā, asuciyā micchādiṭṭhiyā saman‍nāgatāti asucī manussā, asuciyā caitanāya saman‍nāgatāti asucī manussā, asuciyā patthanāya saman‍nāgatāti asucī manussā, asucinā paṇidhinā saman‍nāgatāti asucī manussā, asucī hīnā nihīnā aumakā lāmakā chatuk‍kā parittāti – attatthapañ‍ñā asucī manussā|

aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappauti| aikauti sau pac‍caikasambuddhau pabbaj‍jāsaṅkhātaina aikau…pai॰… caraiti aṭṭha cariyāyau…pai॰… khaggavisāṇakappauti yathā khaggassa nāma visāṇaṃ aikaṃ hauti adutiyaṃ…pai॰… aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau| taināha sau pac‍caikasambuddhau –

‘‘bhajanti  saivanti ca kāraṇatthā, nik‍kāraṇā dul‍labhā aj‍ja mittā|

attatthapañ‍ñā asucī manussā, aikau carai khaggavisāṇakappau’’ti||

catutthau vaggau|

khaggavisāṇasuttaniddaisau niṭṭhitau|


ajitau  tissamaittaiyyau, puṇṇakau atha maittagū|

dhautakau upasīvau ca, nandau ca atha haimakau||

taudaiyya-kappā dubhayau, jatukaṇṇī ca paṇḍitau|

bhadrāvudhau udayau ca, pausālau cāpi brāhmaṇau|

maugharājā ca maidhāvī, piṅgiyau ca mahāisi||

sauḷasānaṃ sauḷasan‍naṃ (syā॰ ka॰) panaitaisaṃ, brāhmaṇānaṃva sāsanaṃ|

pārāyanānaṃ niddaisā, tattakā ca bhavanti hi vā (ka॰)||

khaggavisāṇasuttānaṃ, niddaisāpi tathaiva ca|

niddaisā duvidhā ñaiyyā, paripuṇṇā sulakkhitāti||

cūḷaniddaisapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
